## src/layouts/AdminLayout.tsx
```tsx:src/layouts/AdminLayout.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import {
  LayoutDashboard, Film, DollarSign, Users, Receipt,
  ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Home, Menu, X, LogOut, ChevronDown
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";

const ADMIN_NAV = [
  { label: "Dashboard", to: "/admin", icon: LayoutDashboard, end: true },
  { label: "Content", to: "/admin/content", icon: Film },
  { label: "Pricing", to: "/admin/pricing", icon: DollarSign },
  { label: "Users", to: "/admin/users", icon: Users },
  { label: "Transactions", to: "/admin/transactions", icon: Receipt },
];

export default function AdminLayout() {
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const { currentUser, setMockState } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const sidebar = (
    <aside className={`flex flex-col h-full bg-card border-r border-border transition-all duration-300 ${collapsed ? "w-16" : "w-56"}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border min-h-[65px]">
        {!collapsed && (
          <Link to="/admin" className="text-lg font-black">
            <span className="text-primary">Cine</span>
            <span className="text-foreground">Vault</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground ml-1.5">Admin</span>
          </Link>
        )}
        <button onClick={() => setCollapsed((c) => !c)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
          {collapsed ? <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />}
        </button>
      </div>

      <nav className="flex-1 p-3 space-y-1">
        {ADMIN_NAV.map(({ label, to, icon: Icon, end }) => (
          <NavLink key={to} to={to} end={end}
            className={({ isActive }) => `flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 ${isActive ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/20" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted"} ${collapsed ? "justify-center" : ""}`}>
            <Icon className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            {!collapsed && <span>{label}</span>}
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="p-3 border-t border-border space-y-1">
        <NavLink to="/" className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
          <Home className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
          {!collapsed && <span>Back to CineVault</span>}
        </NavLink>
        {currentUser && (
          <button onClick={() => setMockState("GUEST")} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium text-red-400 hover:bg-muted transition-colors w-full">
            <LogOut className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            {!collapsed && <span>Sign Out</span>}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background font-[Manrope,sans-serif] flex flex-col">
      <Toaster position="top-right" theme="dark" richColors />

      {/* Mobile top bar */}
      <header className="md:hidden sticky top-0 z-40 h-14 flex items-center justify-between px-4 border-b border-border bg-background/90 backdrop-blur-md">
        <Link to="/admin" className="text-lg font-black">
          <span className="text-primary">Cine</span><span className="text-foreground">Vault</span>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ml-1">Admin</span>
        </Link>
        <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen((o) => !o)} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted">
          {mobileOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />}
        </button>
      </header>

      <div className="flex flex-1 min-h-0">
        {/* Desktop sidebar */}
        <div className="hidden md:flex flex-shrink-0">{sidebar}</div>

        {/* Mobile drawer */}
        {mobileOpen && (
          <div className="md:hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 flex">
            <div className="w-56 bg-card border-r border-border flex flex-col">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border">
                <span className="font-black text-lg"><span className="text-primary">Cine</span><span className="text-foreground">Vault</span></span>
                <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}><X className="w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" /></button>
              </div>
              <nav className="flex-1 p-3 space-y-1">
                {ADMIN_NAV.map(({ label, to, icon: Icon, end }) => (
                  <NavLink key={to} to={to} end={end} onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                    className={({ isActive }) => `flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${isActive ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted"}`}>
                    <Icon className="w-4 h-4" />{label}
                  </NavLink>
                ))}
              </nav>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 bg-black/50" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Main content */}
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 overflow-auto">
          {/* Admin header */}
          <header className="h-[65px] border-b border-border px-6 flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
            {currentUser && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-primary/20 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <span className="text-primary text-xs font-bold">{currentUser.name[0]}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{currentUser.name}</span>
                <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/20">Admin</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </header>

          <div className="p-6">
            <Outlet />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/layouts/PublicLayout.tsx
```tsx:src/layouts/PublicLayout.tsx
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Link, NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate, useLocation } from "react-router";
import { Search, Bell, Menu, X, Sun, Moon, Crown, ChevronDown, LogOut, User, Wallet, History, Heart } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { WalletBalanceBadge, PremiumBadge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import type { MockUserState } from "@/types";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";

const NAV_LINKS = [
  { label: "Home", to: "/" },
  { label: "Movies", to: "/movies" },
  { label: "TV Series", to: "/series" },
  { label: "Premium", to: "/subscription" },
  { label: "FAQ", to: "/#faq" },
];

export default function PublicLayout() {
  const { currentUser, isAuthenticated, hasPremium, mockState, setMockState } = useAuth();
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(true);
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [userMenuOpen, setUserMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [devMenuOpen, setDevMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", isDark);
  }, [isDark]);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
    setMobileOpen(false);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  const DEV_STATES: { label: string; value: MockUserState }[] = [
    { label: "Guest", value: "GUEST" },
    { label: "User (Normal)", value: "USER" },
    { label: "User (Premium)", value: "PREMIUM" },
    { label: "Admin", value: "ADMIN" },
  ];

  function handleNavFaq(e: React.MouseEvent) {
    if (location.pathname === "/") {
      e.preventDefault();
      document.getElementById("faq")?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background font-[Manrope,sans-serif]">
      <Toaster position="top-right" theme={isDark ? "dark" : "light"} richColors />

      {/* ── Navbar ── */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-border bg-background/90 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 h-16 flex items-center gap-4">
          {/* Logo */}
          <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 mr-4">
            <span className="text-xl font-black tracking-tight">
              <span className="text-primary">Cine</span>
              <span className="text-foreground">Vault</span>
            </span>
          </Link>

          {/* Desktop nav */}
          <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1 flex-1 justify-center">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              link.to === "/#faq" ? (
                <a key={link.to} href="/#faq" onClick={handleNavFaq}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                  {link.label}
                </a>
              ) : (
                <NavLink key={link.to} to={link.to} end={link.to === "/"}
                  className={({ isActive }) => `px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${isActive ? "text-foreground bg-muted" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted"}`}>
                  {link.label}
                </NavLink>
              )
            ))}
          </nav>

          {/* Right actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 ml-auto">
            <button onClick={() => navigate("/search")} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setIsDark((d) => !d)} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
              {isDark ? <Sun className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Moon className="w-4 h-4" />}
            </button>

            {isAuthenticated ? (
              <>
                {hasPremium && <PremiumBadge className="hidden md:flex" />}
                <WalletBalanceBadge balance={currentUser!.walletBalance} className="hidden md:flex" />
                <button className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors relative">
                  <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary" />
                </button>
                {/* User menu */}
                <div className="relative hidden md:block">
                  <button onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors text-foreground">
                    <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary/20 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <span className="text-primary text-xs font-bold">{currentUser!.name[0]}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-sm font-medium max-w-20 truncate">{currentUser!.name}</span>
                    <ChevronDown className="w-3 h-3 text-muted-foreground" />
                  </button>
                  {userMenuOpen && (
                    <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-48 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border shadow-2xl py-1 z-50">
                      {currentUser?.role === "ADMIN" && (
                        <Link to="/admin" onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                          <User className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" /> Admin Panel
                        </Link>
                      )}
                      <Link to="/profile" onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                        <User className="w-4 h-4" /> Profile
                      </Link>
                      <Link to="/wallet" onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                        <Wallet className="w-4 h-4" /> Wallet
                      </Link>
                      <Link to="/favorites" onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                        <Heart className="w-4 h-4" /> Favorites
                      </Link>
                      <Link to="/history" onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                        <History className="w-4 h-4" /> Watch History
                      </Link>
                      <div className="border-t border-border my-1" />
                      <button onClick={() => { setMockState("GUEST"); setUserMenuOpen(false); }} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-400 hover:bg-muted transition-colors w-full">
                        <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" /> Sign Out
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => setMockState("USER")} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                  Sign In
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => setMockState("USER")} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-primary text-white hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors">
                  Create Account
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Mobile hamburger */}
            <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen((o) => !o)} className="md:hidden w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
              {mobileOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile menu */}
        {mobileOpen && (
          <div className="md:hidden border-t border-border bg-card px-4 py-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              link.to === "/#faq" ? (
                <a key={link.to} href="/#faq" onClick={(e) => { handleNavFaq(e); setMobileOpen(false); }}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                  {link.label}
                </a>
              ) : (
                <NavLink key={link.to} to={link.to} end={link.to === "/"} onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                  className={({ isActive }) => `px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${isActive ? "text-foreground bg-muted" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted"}`}>
                  {link.label}
                </NavLink>
              )
            ))}
            {isAuthenticated ? (
              <>
                <Link to="/wallet" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted">
                  Wallet · {currentUser!.walletBalance.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                </Link>
                <button onClick={() => { setMockState("GUEST"); setMobileOpen(false); }} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-red-400 hover:bg-muted text-left">
                  Sign Out
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <button onClick={() => { setMockState("USER"); setMobileOpen(false); }} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold bg-primary text-white">
                  Sign In / Create Account
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      {/* Dev switcher */}
      <div className="fixed bottom-4 left-4 z-50">
        <div className="relative">
          <button onClick={() => setDevMenuOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-card border border-border shadow-lg text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors">
            Dev: {mockState}
            <ChevronDown className="w-3 h-3" />
          </button>
          {devMenuOpen && (
            <div className="absolute bottom-full mb-2 left-0 w-44 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border shadow-2xl py-1">
              {DEV_STATES.map((s) => (
                <button key={s.value} onClick={() => { setMockState(s.value); setDevMenuOpen(false); }}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-2 w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${mockState === s.value ? "text-primary" : "text-foreground hover:bg-muted"}`}>
                  {mockState === s.value && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary" />}
                  {s.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Page content */}
      <main>
        <Outlet />
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-border bg-card mt-16">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-12">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5 gap-8 mb-8">
            <div className="col-span-2 md:col-span-1">
              <p className="text-xl font-black mb-3">
                <span className="text-primary">Cine</span>
                <span className="text-foreground">Vault</span>
              </p>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs leading-relaxed">Your next story starts here.</p>
              <div className="flex gap-3 mt-4">
                {[{ icon: "F", label: "Facebook" }, { icon: "T", label: "Twitter" }, { icon: "I", label: "Instagram" }].map((s) => (
                  <button key={s.label} aria-label={s.label} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-muted border border-border flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:border-primary/50 transition-colors text-xs font-bold">
                    {s.icon}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            {[
              { title: "Home", links: ["Categories", "Trending", "Popular", "New Releases"] },
              { title: "Movies", links: ["Genres", "Recently Added", "Recommended"] },
              { title: "TV Series", links: ["Genres", "Seasons", "New Episodes"] },
              { title: "Account", links: ["Wallet", "Favorites", "Watch History", "Premium"] },
              { title: "Support", links: ["FAQ", "Contact", "Terms of Use", "Privacy Policy"] },
            ].map((col) => (
              <div key={col.title}>
                <p className="text-foreground font-semibold text-sm mb-3">{col.title}</p>
                <ul className="space-y-2">
                  {col.links.map((l) => (
                    <li key={l}><a href="#" className="text-muted-foreground text-xs hover:text-foreground transition-colors">{l}</a></li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="border-t border-border pt-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            <span>© 2026 CineVault. All rights reserved.</span>
            <div className="flex gap-4">
              {["Terms of Use", "Privacy Policy", "Cookie Policy"].map((t) => (
                <a key={t} href="#" className="hover:text-foreground transition-colors">{t}</a>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## src/mocks/homeData.ts
```typescript:src/mocks/homeData.ts
import {
  p00, p01, p02, p03, p04, p05, p06, p07, p08,
  p09, p10, p11, p12, p13, p14, p15, p16, p17,
  p18, p19, p20, p21, p22, p23, p24, p25, p26,
  p27, p28, p29, p30, p31, p32, p33, p34, p35,
  c00, c03, c04, c05, c06, c07, c08, c09, c10,
  c11, c12, c13, c14, c15, c16, c17, c18, c19,
  ctaBg,
} from "@/app/images";

export const HERO_ROW_1 = [p00, p01, p02, p03, p04, p05, p06, p07, p08];
export const HERO_ROW_2 = [p09, p10, p11, p12, p13, p14, p15, p16, p17];
export const HERO_ROW_3 = [p18, p19, p20, p21, p22, p23, p24, p25, p26];
export const HERO_ROW_4 = [p27, p28, p29, p30, p31, p32, p33, p34, p35];
```

## src/pages/admin/AdminContentPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminContentPage.tsx
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Search, Edit, Eye, EyeOff, Lock, Unlock, Film, Tv } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import type { Movie, PublicationStatus, AccessMode } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

type ContentItem = { id: string; title: string; type: "MOVIE" | "SERIES"; genres: string[]; releaseYear: number; publicationStatus: PublicationStatus; accessMode: AccessMode; resolvedPrice: number; popularity: number; };

export default function AdminContentPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<"ALL" | "MOVIE" | "SERIES">("ALL");
  const [pubFilter, setPubFilter] = useState<"ALL" | "PUBLISHED" | "HIDDEN">("ALL");
  const [accessFilter, setAccessFilter] = useState<"ALL" | "FREE" | "LOCKED">("ALL");
  const [statuses, setStatuses] = useState<Record<string, PublicationStatus>>({});
  const [accessModes, setAccessModes] = useState<Record<string, AccessMode>>({});

  const allItems: ContentItem[] = [
    ...MOCK_MOVIES.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, title: m.title, type: "MOVIE" as const, genres: m.genres, releaseYear: m.releaseYear, publicationStatus: statuses[m.id] ?? m.publicationStatus, accessMode: accessModes[m.id] ?? m.accessMode, resolvedPrice: m.resolvedPrice, popularity: m.popularity })),
    ...MOCK_SERIES.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, title: s.title, type: "SERIES" as const, genres: s.genres, releaseYear: s.releaseYear, publicationStatus: statuses[s.id] ?? s.publicationStatus, accessMode: accessModes[s.id] ?? s.defaultAccessMode, resolvedPrice: 0, popularity: s.popularity })),
  ];

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return allItems.filter((item) => {
      if (search && !item.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())) return false;
      if (typeFilter !== "ALL" && item.type !== typeFilter) return false;
      if (pubFilter !== "ALL" && item.publicationStatus !== pubFilter) return false;
      if (accessFilter !== "ALL" && item.accessMode !== accessFilter) return false;
      return true;
    }).sort((a, b) => b.popularity - a.popularity);
  }, [search, typeFilter, pubFilter, accessFilter, allItems]);

  function togglePub(id: string, current: PublicationStatus) {
    const next: PublicationStatus = current === "PUBLISHED" ? "HIDDEN" : "PUBLISHED";
    setStatuses((p) => ({ ...p, [id]: next }));
    toast.success(`Content ${next === "PUBLISHED" ? "published" : "hidden"}`);
  }

  function toggleAccess(id: string, current: AccessMode) {
    const next: AccessMode = current === "FREE" ? "LOCKED" : "FREE";
    setAccessModes((p) => ({ ...p, [id]: next }));
    toast.success(`Access changed to ${next}`);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 flex-wrap gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">Content Management</h1>
        <button className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-primary text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors">
          Import from TMDb
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
          <input value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search title..." className="pl-9 pr-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 w-56" />
        </div>
        {[
          { label: "Type", value: typeFilter, set: setTypeFilter, options: ["ALL", "MOVIE", "SERIES"] },
          { label: "Status", value: pubFilter, set: setPubFilter, options: ["ALL", "PUBLISHED", "HIDDEN"] },
          { label: "Access", value: accessFilter, set: setAccessFilter, options: ["ALL", "FREE", "LOCKED"] },
        ].map(({ label, value, set, options }) => (
          <select key={label} value={value} onChange={(e) => (set as (v: string) => void)(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50">
            {options.map((o) => <option key={o} value={o}>{o === "ALL" ? `All ${label}s` : o}</option>)}
          </select>
        ))}
        <span className="ml-auto text-sm text-muted-foreground self-center">{filtered.length} items</span>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[700px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-border">
                {["Title", "Type", "Genres", "Year", "Status", "Access", "Price", "Actions"].map((h) => (
                  <th key={h} className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground whitespace-nowrap">{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((item) => (
                <tr key={`${item.type}-${item.id}`} className="border-b border-border/50 hover:bg-muted/20">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {item.type === "MOVIE" ? <Film className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0" /> : <Tv className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0" />}
                      <span className="text-foreground font-medium truncate max-w-[180px]">{item.title}</span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground">{item.type}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{item.genres.slice(0, 2).join(", ")}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground">{item.releaseYear}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${item.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED" ? "bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-400" : "bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-400"}`}>
                      {item.publicationStatus}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${item.accessMode === "FREE" ? "bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-400" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                      {item.accessMode}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground text-xs">
                    {item.accessMode === "LOCKED" && item.resolvedPrice > 0 ? `${item.resolvedPrice.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND` : "—"}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      {item.type === "MOVIE" ? (
                        <Link to={`/admin/movies/${item.id}`} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors" title="Edit">
                          <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />
                        </Link>
                      ) : (
                        <Link to={`/admin/series/${item.id}`} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors" title="Edit">
                          <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />
                        </Link>
                      )}
                      <button onClick={() => togglePub(item.id, item.publicationStatus)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors" title={item.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED" ? "Hide" : "Publish"}>
                        {item.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED" ? <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" /> : <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />}
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => toggleAccess(item.id, item.accessMode)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors" title={item.accessMode === "LOCKED" ? "Make Free" : "Lock"}>
                        {item.accessMode === "LOCKED" ? <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" /> : <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/admin/AdminDashboardPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminDashboardPage.tsx
import { Users, Film, Crown, Receipt, TrendingUp, Tv } from "lucide-react";
import { BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, LineChart, Line } from "recharts";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import { MOCK_USERS } from "@/mocks/users";
import { MOCK_TRANSACTIONS } from "@/mocks/transactions";

const revenueData = [
  { month: "Mar", revenue: 450000 },
  { month: "Apr", revenue: 620000 },
  { month: "May", revenue: 580000 },
  { month: "Jun", revenue: 790000 },
  { month: "Jul", revenue: 860000 },
  { month: "Aug", revenue: 1020000 },
  { month: "Sep", revenue: 940000 },
];

const topMovies = [
  { title: "Kantara", unlocks: 412 },
  { title: "Inception", unlocks: 387 },
  { title: "The Dark Knight", unlocks: 344 },
  { title: "Interstellar", unlocks: 291 },
  { title: "Avengers: Endgame", unlocks: 256 },
];

function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, sub, color = "primary" }: { icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>; label: string; value: string | number; sub?: string; color?: string }) {
  const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    primary: "bg-primary/10 text-primary border-primary/20",
    amber: "bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-400 border-amber-700/30",
    emerald: "bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-700/30",
    blue: "bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-400 border-blue-700/30",
  };
  return (
    <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
      <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-center mb-3 ${colors[color]}`}>
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5" />
      </div>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mb-1">{label}</p>
      <p className="text-2xl font-black text-foreground">{value}</p>
      {sub && <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-0.5">{sub}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const totalUsers = MOCK_USERS.length;
  const premiumUsers = MOCK_USERS.filter((u) => u.premium.status === "ACTIVE").length;
  const totalMovies = MOCK_MOVIES.length;
  const totalSeries = MOCK_SERIES.length;
  const totalEpisodes = MOCK_SERIES.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.seasons.reduce((a, s) => a + s.episodes.length, 0), 0);
  const totalTx = MOCK_TRANSACTIONS.length;
  const revenue = MOCK_TRANSACTIONS.filter((t) => t.amount > 0 && t.status === "COMPLETED").reduce((acc, t) => acc + t.amount, 0);

  const recentTx = MOCK_TRANSACTIONS.slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-6">Dashboard</h1>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-8">
        <StatCard icon={Users} label="Total Users" value={totalUsers} color="blue" />
        <StatCard icon={Crown} label="Premium Users" value={premiumUsers} sub={`${Math.round((premiumUsers / totalUsers) * 100)}% of users`} color="amber" />
        <StatCard icon={Film} label="Movies" value={totalMovies} />
        <StatCard icon={Tv} label="TV Series" value={totalSeries} sub={`${totalEpisodes} episodes`} color="emerald" />
        <StatCard icon={Receipt} label="Transactions" value={totalTx} />
        <StatCard icon={TrendingUp} label="Simulated Revenue" value={`${(revenue / 1000).toFixed(0)}K VND`} color="emerald" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-8">
        {/* Revenue chart */}
        <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Revenue (VND)</h2>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={200}>
            <LineChart data={revenueData}>
              <XAxis dataKey="month" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "var(--muted-foreground)" }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
              <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "var(--muted-foreground)" }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} tickFormatter={(v) => `${v / 1000}K`} />
              <Tooltip formatter={(v) => [`${Number(v).toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`, "Revenue"]} contentStyle={{ background: "var(--card)", border: "1px solid var(--border)", borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 12 }} />
              <Line type="monotone" dataKey="revenue" stroke="var(--primary)" strokeWidth={2.5} dot={false} activeDot={{ r: 4, fill: "var(--primary)" }} />
            </LineChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>

        {/* Top movies */}
        <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Most Unlocked Movies</h2>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={200}>
            <BarChart data={topMovies} layout="vertical">
              <XAxis type="number" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "var(--muted-foreground)" }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
              <YAxis type="category" dataKey="title" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "var(--muted-foreground)" }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} width={100} />
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{ background: "var(--card)", border: "1px solid var(--border)", borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 12 }} />
              <Bar dataKey="unlocks" fill="var(--primary)" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Recent transactions */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-foreground">Recent Transactions</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-border">
                {["ID", "User", "Type", "Amount", "Status", "Date"].map((h) => (
                  <th key={h} className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {recentTx.map((tx) => (
                <tr key={tx.id} className="border-b border-border/50 hover:bg-muted/20">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs">{tx.id}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground">{tx.userId}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground capitalize">{tx.type.replace(/_/g, " ").toLowerCase()}</td>
                  <td className={`px-4 py-3 font-semibold ${tx.amount > 0 ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-red-400"}`}>
                    {tx.amount > 0 ? "+" : ""}{tx.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${tx.status === "COMPLETED" ? "bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-400" : tx.status === "FAILED" ? "bg-red-900/40 text-red-400" : "bg-amber-900/40 text-amber-400"}`}>
                      {tx.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## src/pages/admin/AdminMovieEditorPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminMovieEditorPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { ChevronLeft, Save, Lock, Unlock, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { MOCK_MOVIES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import { MOCK_PRICE_PRESETS } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import type { PublicationStatus, AccessMode, PriceSource } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export default function AdminMovieEditorPage() {
  const { movieId } = useParams<{ movieId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const movie = MOCK_MOVIES.find((m) => m.id === movieId);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    publicationStatus: (movie?.publicationStatus ?? "PUBLISHED") as PublicationStatus,
    playbackUrl: movie?.playbackUrl ?? "",
    trailerUrl: movie?.trailerYoutubeId ? `https://youtube.com/watch?v=${movie.trailerYoutubeId}` : "",
    accessMode: (movie?.accessMode ?? "FREE") as AccessMode,
    priceSource: (movie?.priceSource ?? "PRESET_1") as PriceSource,
    customPrice: String(movie?.customPrice ?? ""),
    unlockDurationHours: String(movie?.unlockDurationHours ?? 24),
  });

  const presets = MOCK_PRICE_PRESETS.filter((p) => p.isActive);

  function resolvedPrice() {
    if (form.priceSource === "PRESET_1") return presets[0]?.amount ?? 0;
    if (form.priceSource === "PRESET_2") return presets[1]?.amount ?? 0;
    return Number(form.customPrice) || 0;
  }

  function handleSave() {
    if (form.accessMode === "LOCKED" && resolvedPrice() <= 0) { toast.error("Set a valid price for locked content"); return; }
    toast.success("Movie settings saved");
    navigate("/admin/content");
  }

  if (!movie) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center py-16">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Movie not found.</p>
        <button onClick={() => navigate("/admin/content")} className="text-primary hover:underline text-sm mt-2">Back to Content</button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl">
      <button onClick={() => navigate(-1)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-sm mb-6 transition-colors">
        <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Back to Content
      </button>

      <div className="flex items-start gap-4 mb-8">
        <img src={movie.posterUrl} alt={movie.title} className="w-20 rounded-xl object-cover aspect-[2/3] border border-border flex-shrink-0" />
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">{movie.title}</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">{movie.releaseYear} · {movie.director}</p>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-1 line-clamp-2">{movie.overview}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {/* Publication */}
        <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mb-3">Publication</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {(["PUBLISHED", "HIDDEN"] as const).map((s) => (
              <button key={s} onClick={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, publicationStatus: s }))} className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${form.publicationStatus === s ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
                {s === "PUBLISHED" ? <Eye className="w-4 h-4" /> : <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" />}
                {s}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Playback */}
        <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mb-3">Playback & Trailer</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Playback URL</label>
              <input value={form.playbackUrl} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, playbackUrl: e.target.value }))} placeholder="https://..." className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Trailer YouTube URL</label>
              <input value={form.trailerUrl} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, trailerUrl: e.target.value }))} placeholder="https://youtube.com/watch?v=..." className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Access */}
        <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mb-3">Access Settings</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            {(["FREE", "LOCKED"] as const).map((a) => (
              <button key={a} onClick={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, accessMode: a }))} className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${form.accessMode === a ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
                {a === "FREE" ? <Unlock className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Lock className="w-4 h-4" />}
                {a}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {form.accessMode === "LOCKED" && (
            <div className="space-y-3 pt-3 border-t border-border">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Price Source</label>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {([
                    { label: presets[0] ? `${presets[0].name} (${presets[0].amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND)` : "Default Price 1", value: "PRESET_1" as PriceSource },
                    { label: presets[1] ? `${presets[1].name} (${presets[1].amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND)` : "Default Price 2", value: "PRESET_2" as PriceSource },
                    { label: "Custom Price", value: "CUSTOM" as PriceSource },
                  ]).map(({ label, value }) => (
                    <button key={value} onClick={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, priceSource: value }))} disabled={value === "PRESET_2" && !presets[1]} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-colors disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed ${form.priceSource === value ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
                      {label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              {form.priceSource === "CUSTOM" && (
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Custom Price (VND)</label>
                  <input type="number" value={form.customPrice} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, customPrice: e.target.value }))} placeholder="e.g. 5000" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
                </div>
              )}
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Unlock Duration</label>
                <input type="number" value={form.unlockDurationHours} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, unlockDurationHours: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Hours" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
                <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-1">{Number(form.unlockDurationHours) >= 24 ? `${Math.round(Number(form.unlockDurationHours) / 24)} day${Math.round(Number(form.unlockDurationHours) / 24) !== 1 ? "s" : ""}` : `${form.unlockDurationHours} hours`}</p>
              </div>

              {/* Preview */}
              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-border">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground mb-2">Preview: User-facing locked card</p>
                <div className="relative w-28 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border">
                  <img src={movie.posterUrl} alt="" className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover opacity-60" />
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1">
                    <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
                    <span className="text-white text-xs font-semibold bg-black/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      {resolvedPrice().toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate(-1)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleSave}><Save className="w-4 h-4" /> Save Changes</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/admin/AdminPricingPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminPricingPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Edit, Trash2, AlertTriangle, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { Modal, ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { MOCK_PRICE_PRESETS } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import type { PricePreset } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export default function AdminPricingPage() {
  const [presets, setPresets] = useState<PricePreset[]>(MOCK_PRICE_PRESETS);
  const [editTarget, setEditTarget] = useState<PricePreset | null>(null);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<PricePreset | null>(null);
  const [addOpen, setAddOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", amount: "" });

  const active = presets.filter((p) => p.isActive);
  const maxReached = active.length >= 2;

  function savePreset() {
    if (!form.name || !form.amount || Number(form.amount) <= 0) { toast.error("Enter a valid name and amount"); return; }
    if (editTarget) {
      setPresets((p) => p.map((x) => x.id === editTarget.id ? { ...x, name: form.name, amount: Number(form.amount) } : x));
      toast.success("Preset updated");
    } else {
      if (maxReached) { toast.error("Maximum 2 active presets reached"); return; }
      setPresets((p) => [...p, { id: `pp${Date.now()}`, name: form.name, amount: Number(form.amount), isActive: true }]);
      toast.success("Preset created");
    }
    setEditTarget(null);
    setAddOpen(false);
    setForm({ name: "", amount: "" });
  }

  function confirmDelete() {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    setPresets((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== deleteTarget.id));
    toast.success("Preset deleted. Existing content retains its latest price as a custom price.");
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  }

  function openEdit(p: PricePreset) { setEditTarget(p); setForm({ name: p.name, amount: String(p.amount) }); setAddOpen(true); }
  function openAdd() { setEditTarget(null); setForm({ name: "", amount: "" }); setAddOpen(true); }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">Pricing Settings</h1>
        <Button onClick={openAdd} disabled={maxReached} title={maxReached ? "Maximum 2 active presets reached" : undefined}>
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Add Price Preset
        </Button>
      </div>

      {maxReached && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/20 border border-amber-700/30 mb-6">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          <p className="text-amber-200 text-sm">Maximum of 2 active price presets reached. Delete one before adding another.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-8">
        {presets.map((preset) => (
          <div key={preset.id} className={`rounded-2xl border bg-card p-6 ${preset.isActive ? "border-primary/30" : "border-border opacity-60"}`}>
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-3">
              <div>
                <p className="text-foreground font-bold text-lg">{preset.name}</p>
                {preset.isActive && (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-400 font-semibold">
                    <Check className="w-3 h-3" /> Active
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => openEdit(preset)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors">
                  <Edit className="w-4 h-4 text-foreground" />
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(preset)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-red-900/30 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors">
                  <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-foreground" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="text-3xl font-black text-foreground">{preset.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} <span className="text-base font-medium text-muted-foreground">VND</span></p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-foreground mb-3">Price Selector Logic</h2>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm leading-relaxed">
          When editing a movie or episode, admins can select from <strong className="text-foreground">Default Price 1</strong>, <strong className="text-foreground">Default Price 2</strong>, or a <strong className="text-foreground">Custom Price</strong>. Changing a preset amount does not retroactively change completed transactions — those retain their original price snapshot.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Add/Edit modal */}
      <Modal open={addOpen} onClose={() => setAddOpen(false)} title={editTarget ? "Edit Price Preset" : "Add Price Preset"} size="sm">
        <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">Preset Name</label>
            <input value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value }))} placeholder="e.g. Default Price 1" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">Amount (VND)</label>
            <input type="number" value={form.amount} onChange={(e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, amount: e.target.value }))} placeholder="e.g. 3000" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Button fullWidth variant="ghost" onClick={() => setAddOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button fullWidth onClick={savePreset}>{editTarget ? "Save Changes" : "Create Preset"}</Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!deleteTarget}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        onConfirm={confirmDelete}
        title="Delete Price Preset"
        description={`Delete "${deleteTarget?.name}"? Existing content will retain its latest amount as a custom price. Previously completed transactions keep their original price snapshots.`}
        confirmLabel="Delete"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/admin/AdminSeasonEditorPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminSeasonEditorPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { ChevronLeft, Save, Lock, Unlock, Eye, EyeOff, AlertTriangle, Edit } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { ConfirmDialog, Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import { MOCK_PRICE_PRESETS } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import type { Episode, PublicationStatus, AccessMode, PriceSource, EpisodeSettingSource } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export default function AdminSeasonEditorPage() {
  const { seriesId, seasonId } = useParams<{ seriesId: string; seasonId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const series = MOCK_SERIES.find((s) => s.id === seriesId);
  const season = series?.seasons.find((s) => s.id === seasonId);
  const presets = MOCK_PRICE_PRESETS.filter((p) => p.isActive);

  const [applyConfirmMode, setApplyConfirmMode] = useState<"future" | "inheriting" | "all" | null>(null);
  const [applyDialogOpen, setApplyDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [episodes, setEpisodes] = useState<Episode[]>(season?.episodes ?? []);
  const [editingEp, setEditingEp] = useState<Episode | null>(null);
  const [epForm, setEpForm] = useState({ accessMode: "FREE" as AccessMode, priceSource: "PRESET_1" as PriceSource, customPrice: "", unlockDurationHours: "24", publicationStatus: "PUBLISHED" as PublicationStatus });

  const [seasonForm, setSeasonForm] = useState({
    defaultAccessMode: season?.defaultAccessMode ?? "FREE" as AccessMode,
    defaultPriceSource: season?.defaultPriceSource ?? "PRESET_1" as PriceSource,
    defaultCustomPrice: String(season?.defaultCustomPrice ?? ""),
    defaultUnlockDurationHours: String(season?.defaultUnlockDurationHours ?? 24),
    publicationStatus: season?.publicationStatus ?? "PUBLISHED" as PublicationStatus,
  });

  if (!series || !season) {
    return <div className="text-center py-16"><p className="text-muted-foreground">Season not found.</p></div>;
  }

  function openEditEp(ep: Episode) {
    setEditingEp(ep);
    setEpForm({ accessMode: ep.accessMode, priceSource: ep.priceSource, customPrice: String(ep.customPrice ?? ""), unlockDurationHours: String(ep.unlockDurationHours), publicationStatus: ep.publicationStatus });
  }

  function saveEpisode() {
    if (!editingEp) return;
    setEpisodes((prev) => prev.map((e) => e.id === editingEp.id ? {
      ...e, ...epForm, resolvedPrice: epForm.priceSource === "PRESET_1" ? presets[0]?.amount ?? 0 : epForm.priceSource === "PRESET_2" ? presets[1]?.amount ?? 0 : Number(epForm.customPrice), settingSource: "OVERRIDE" as EpisodeSettingSource,
    } : e));
    setEditingEp(null);
    toast.success("Episode updated");
  }

  function applyToAll(mode: "future" | "inheriting" | "all") {
    setEpisodes((prev) => prev.map((e) => {
      if (mode === "future") return e;
      if (mode === "inheriting" && e.settingSource !== "SEASON") return e;
      return {
        ...e, accessMode: seasonForm.defaultAccessMode, priceSource: seasonForm.defaultPriceSource, unlockDurationHours: Number(seasonForm.defaultUnlockDurationHours),
        resolvedPrice: seasonForm.defaultPriceSource === "PRESET_1" ? presets[0]?.amount ?? 0 : seasonForm.defaultPriceSource === "PRESET_2" ? presets[1]?.amount ?? 0 : Number(seasonForm.defaultCustomPrice),
        settingSource: "SEASON" as EpisodeSettingSource,
      };
    }));
    toast.success("Season defaults applied");
    setApplyDialogOpen(false);
    setApplyConfirmMode(null);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <button onClick={() => navigate(-1)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-sm mb-6 transition-colors">
        <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Back
      </button>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-1">{series.title}</h1>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-6">{season.title} · {season.episodes.length} episodes</p>

      {/* Season defaults */}
      <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Season Default Settings</h2>
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Default Access</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {(["FREE", "LOCKED"] as const).map((a) => (
                <button key={a} onClick={() => setSeasonForm((f) => ({ ...f, defaultAccessMode: a }))} className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${seasonForm.defaultAccessMode === a ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                  {a === "FREE" ? <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}{a}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Default Unlock Duration (hours)</label>
            <input type="number" value={seasonForm.defaultUnlockDurationHours} onChange={(e) => setSeasonForm((f) => ({ ...f, defaultUnlockDurationHours: e.target.value }))} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none" />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button onClick={() => setApplyDialogOpen(true)} variant="secondary" size="sm">Apply to All Episodes</Button>
      </div>

      {/* Episode table */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden mb-6">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[600px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-border">
                {["#", "Title", "Status", "Access", "Price", "Duration", "Source", "Actions"].map((h) => (
                  <th key={h} className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {episodes.map((ep) => (
                <tr key={ep.id} className="border-b border-border/50 hover:bg-muted/20">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{ep.episodeNumber}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground font-medium text-xs max-w-[160px] truncate">{ep.title}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${ep.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED" ? "bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-400" : "bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-400"}`}>{ep.publicationStatus}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ${ep.accessMode === "FREE" ? "bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-400" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>{ep.accessMode}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground text-xs">{ep.accessMode === "LOCKED" ? `${ep.resolvedPrice.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND` : "—"}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{ep.unlockDurationHours}h</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ${ep.settingSource === "SEASON" ? "bg-muted text-muted-foreground" : "bg-primary/15 text-primary"}`}>{ep.settingSource}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <button onClick={() => openEditEp(ep)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors">
                      <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Apply dialog */}
      <Modal open={applyDialogOpen} onClose={() => setApplyDialogOpen(false)} title="Apply Season Defaults" size="sm">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-4">Choose how to apply the season default settings:</p>
        <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
          {[
            { mode: "future" as const, label: "Apply only to future episodes", desc: "Existing episodes are not changed." },
            { mode: "inheriting" as const, label: "Update episodes inheriting season settings", desc: "Only episodes with source = SEASON are updated." },
            { mode: "all" as const, label: "Override all episodes", desc: "All episodes, including manually edited ones, will be overridden.", warning: true },
          ].map(({ mode, label, desc, warning }) => (
            <button key={mode} onClick={() => setApplyConfirmMode(mode)} className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-xl border transition-colors ${applyConfirmMode === mode ? "border-primary bg-primary/10" : "border-border bg-muted hover:border-primary/40"}`}>
              <p className="text-foreground text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                {warning && <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />}{label}
              </p>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-0.5">{desc}</p>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Button fullWidth variant="ghost" onClick={() => setApplyDialogOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button fullWidth disabled={!applyConfirmMode} onClick={() => applyConfirmMode && applyToAll(applyConfirmMode)}>Apply</Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      {/* Episode edit modal */}
      <Modal open={!!editingEp} onClose={() => setEditingEp(null)} title={`Edit Episode ${editingEp?.episodeNumber}`} size="sm">
        <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Publication Status</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {(["PUBLISHED", "HIDDEN"] as const).map((s) => (
                <button key={s} onClick={() => setEpForm((f) => ({ ...f, publicationStatus: s }))} className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${epForm.publicationStatus === s ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                  {s === "PUBLISHED" ? <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}{s}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Access Mode</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {(["FREE", "LOCKED"] as const).map((a) => (
                <button key={a} onClick={() => setEpForm((f) => ({ ...f, accessMode: a }))} className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${epForm.accessMode === a ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                  {a === "FREE" ? <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}{a}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          {epForm.accessMode === "LOCKED" && (
            <>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Price Source</label>
                <select value={epForm.priceSource} onChange={(e) => setEpForm((f) => ({ ...f, priceSource: e.target.value as PriceSource }))} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
                  {presets[0] && <option value="PRESET_1">{presets[0].name} ({presets[0].amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND)</option>}
                  {presets[1] && <option value="PRESET_2">{presets[1].name} ({presets[1].amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND)</option>}
                  <option value="CUSTOM">Custom Price</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              {epForm.priceSource === "CUSTOM" && (
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Custom Price (VND)</label>
                  <input type="number" value={epForm.customPrice} onChange={(e) => setEpForm((f) => ({ ...f, customPrice: e.target.value }))} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none" />
                </div>
              )}
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground block mb-1.5">Unlock Duration (hours)</label>
                <input type="number" value={epForm.unlockDurationHours} onChange={(e) => setEpForm((f) => ({ ...f, unlockDurationHours: e.target.value }))} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none" />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Button fullWidth variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditingEp(null)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button fullWidth onClick={saveEpisode}><Save className="w-4 h-4" /> Save Episode</Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## src/pages/admin/AdminTransactionsPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminTransactionsPage.tsx
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Search, ChevronRight, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { MOCK_TRANSACTIONS } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import { MOCK_USERS } from "@/mocks/users";
import type { WalletTransaction } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

const TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  TOP_UP: "Top Up", MOVIE_UNLOCK: "Movie Unlock", EPISODE_UNLOCK: "Episode Unlock",
  SUBSCRIPTION_PAYMENT: "Premium Payment", ADMIN_ADJUSTMENT: "Admin Adjustment",
};

export default function AdminTransactionsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("");
  const [detailTx, setDetailTx] = useState<WalletTransaction | null>(null);
  const [adjustOpen, setAdjustOpen] = useState(false);
  const [adjustUser, setAdjustUser] = useState("");
  const [adjustDir, setAdjustDir] = useState<"increase" | "decrease">("increase");
  const [adjustAmt, setAdjustAmt] = useState("");
  const [adjustReason, setAdjustReason] = useState("");
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return MOCK_TRANSACTIONS.filter((t) => {
      if (search && !t.id.includes(search) && !t.userId.includes(search)) return false;
      if (typeFilter && t.type !== typeFilter) return false;
      if (statusFilter && t.status !== statusFilter) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [search, typeFilter, statusFilter]);

  function handleAdjust() {
    if (!adjustUser || !adjustAmt || Number(adjustAmt) <= 0 || !adjustReason) { toast.error("Fill all fields"); return; }
    setProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.success(`Wallet adjusted for ${adjustUser}`);
      setAdjustOpen(false); setAdjustUser(""); setAdjustAmt(""); setAdjustReason(""); setAdjustDir("increase");
      setProcessing(false);
    }, 800);
  }

  const getUserName = (userId: string) => MOCK_USERS.find((u) => u.id === userId)?.name ?? userId;

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 flex-wrap gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">Transaction Management</h1>
        <Button onClick={() => setAdjustOpen(true)} variant="secondary">Admin Adjustment</Button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
          <input value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Transaction ID or user..." className="pl-9 pr-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 w-56" />
        </div>
        <select value={typeFilter} onChange={(e) => setTypeFilter(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
          <option value="">All Types</option>
          {Object.entries(TYPE_LABELS).map(([v, l]) => <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
          <option value="">All Statuses</option>
          <option value="COMPLETED">Completed</option>
          <option value="FAILED">Failed</option>
          <option value="PENDING">Pending</option>
        </select>
        <span className="ml-auto text-sm text-muted-foreground self-center">{filtered.length} transactions</span>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[700px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-border">
                {["ID", "User", "Type", "Amount", "Balance After", "Status", "Date", ""].map((h, i) => (
                  <th key={i} className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((tx) => (
                <tr key={tx.id} className="border-b border-border/50 hover:bg-muted/20">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs">{tx.id}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground text-sm">{getUserName(tx.userId)}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground text-xs">{TYPE_LABELS[tx.type] ?? tx.type}</td>
                  <td className={`px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-sm ${tx.amount > 0 ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-red-400"}`}>
                    {tx.amount > 0 ? "+" : ""}{tx.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground text-sm">{tx.balanceAfter.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${tx.status === "COMPLETED" ? "bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-400" : tx.status === "FAILED" ? "bg-red-900/40 text-red-400" : "bg-amber-900/40 text-amber-400"}`}>
                      {tx.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <button onClick={() => setDetailTx(tx)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors">
                      <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Detail modal */}
      <Modal open={!!detailTx} onClose={() => setDetailTx(null)} title="Transaction Details" size="sm">
        {detailTx && (
          <div className="space-y-3 text-sm">
            {[
              ["ID", detailTx.id], ["User", getUserName(detailTx.userId)], ["Type", TYPE_LABELS[detailTx.type]],
              ["Status", detailTx.status], ["Amount", `${detailTx.amount > 0 ? "+" : ""}${detailTx.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
              ["Balance Before", `${detailTx.balanceBefore.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
              ["Balance After", `${detailTx.balanceAfter.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
              detailTx.relatedContentTitle ? ["Content", detailTx.relatedContentTitle] : null,
              detailTx.purchasedDurationHours ? ["Duration", `${detailTx.purchasedDurationHours}h`] : null,
              detailTx.reason ? ["Reason", detailTx.reason] : null,
              ["Date", new Date(detailTx.createdAt).toLocaleString()],
            ].filter(Boolean).map(([k, v]) => (
              <div key={k} className="flex justify-between gap-4">
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">{k}</span>
                <span className="text-foreground font-medium text-right break-all">{v}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>

      {/* Admin adjustment modal */}
      <Modal open={adjustOpen} onClose={() => setAdjustOpen(false)} title="Admin Wallet Adjustment" size="sm">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/20 border border-amber-700/30 mb-5">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
          <p className="text-amber-200 text-xs leading-relaxed">This will directly modify a user's wallet balance. No refund button is available — adjustments are for exceptional cases only.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">User</label>
            <select value={adjustUser} onChange={(e) => setAdjustUser(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
              <option value="">Select user...</option>
              {MOCK_USERS.map((u) => <option key={u.id} value={u.id}>{u.name} ({u.email})</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">Direction</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {(["increase", "decrease"] as const).map((d) => (
                <button key={d} onClick={() => setAdjustDir(d)} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${adjustDir === d ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
                  {d.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + d.slice(1)}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">Amount (VND)</label>
            <input type="number" value={adjustAmt} onChange={(e) => setAdjustAmt(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. 10000" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">Reason</label>
            <textarea value={adjustReason} onChange={(e) => setAdjustReason(e.target.value)} placeholder="Reason for adjustment..." rows={2} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm resize-none" />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Button fullWidth variant="ghost" onClick={() => setAdjustOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button fullWidth loading={processing} onClick={handleAdjust}>Confirm Adjustment</Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}
```
