## src/pages/admin/AdminUsersPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/admin/AdminUsersPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Search, Lock, Unlock, Crown, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { MOCK_USERS } from "@/mocks/users";
import { MOCK_TRANSACTIONS } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import type { User } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export default function AdminUsersPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState("");
  const [premFilter, setPremFilter] = useState("");
  const [statuses, setStatuses] = useState<Record<string, "ACTIVE" | "LOCKED">>({});
  const [selectedUser, setSelectedUser] = useState<User | null>(null);

  const filtered = MOCK_USERS.filter((u) => {
    if (search && !u.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) && !u.email.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())) return false;
    if (roleFilter && u.role !== roleFilter) return false;
    if (premFilter && u.premium.status !== premFilter) return false;
    return true;
  });

  function toggleLock(u: User) {
    const current = statuses[u.id] ?? u.accountStatus;
    const next = current === "ACTIVE" ? "LOCKED" : "ACTIVE";
    setStatuses((p) => ({ ...p, [u.id]: next }));
    toast.success(`Account ${next === "LOCKED" ? "locked" : "unlocked"}`);
  }

  const userTx = selectedUser ? MOCK_TRANSACTIONS.filter((t) => t.userId === selectedUser.id) : [];

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-6">User Management</h1>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
          <input value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Name or email..." className="pl-9 pr-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 w-56" />
        </div>
        <select value={roleFilter} onChange={(e) => setRoleFilter(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
          <option value="">All Roles</option>
          <option value="USER">User</option>
          <option value="ADMIN">Admin</option>
        </select>
        <select value={premFilter} onChange={(e) => setPremFilter(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
          <option value="">All Premium</option>
          <option value="ACTIVE">Premium Active</option>
          <option value="EXPIRED">Premium Expired</option>
          <option value="NONE">No Premium</option>
        </select>
        <span className="ml-auto text-sm text-muted-foreground self-center">{filtered.length} users</span>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[600px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-border">
                {["Name", "Email", "Role", "Premium", "Status", "Balance", "Actions"].map((h) => (
                  <th key={h} className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((u) => {
                const accStatus = statuses[u.id] ?? u.accountStatus;
                return (
                  <tr key={u.id} className="border-b border-border/50 hover:bg-muted/20">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-primary/20 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                          <span className="text-primary text-xs font-bold">{u.name[0]}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-foreground font-medium">{u.name}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{u.email}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3"><span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-muted text-foreground">{u.role}</span></td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      {u.premium.status === "ACTIVE" ? (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-amber-400"><Crown className="w-3 h-3" />Active</span>
                      ) : u.premium.status === "EXPIRED" ? (
                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Expired</span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">—</span>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${accStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-400" : "bg-red-900/40 text-red-400"}`}>
                        {accStatus}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-foreground text-xs">{u.walletBalance.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <button onClick={() => toggleLock(u)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors" title={accStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "Lock account" : "Unlock account"}>
                          {accStatus === "ACTIVE" ? <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" /> : <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />}
                        </button>
                        <button onClick={() => setSelectedUser(u)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-muted flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors" title="View details">
                          <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-foreground" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* User detail drawer */}
      <Modal open={!!selectedUser} onClose={() => setSelectedUser(null)} title={selectedUser?.name ?? ""} size="md">
        {selectedUser && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
              {[
                ["Email", selectedUser.email],
                ["Role", selectedUser.role],
                ["Status", statuses[selectedUser.id] ?? selectedUser.accountStatus],
                ["Wallet", `${selectedUser.walletBalance.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
                ["Premium", selectedUser.premium.status],
                ["Member Since", new Date(selectedUser.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()],
              ].map(([k, v]) => (
                <div key={k} className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-muted">
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mb-0.5">{k}</p>
                  <p className="text-foreground font-medium text-sm">{v}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-foreground font-semibold text-sm mb-2">Recent Transactions</p>
              {userTx.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">No transactions.</p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
                  {userTx.slice(0, 8).map((tx) => (
                    <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-lg bg-muted text-xs">
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground">{tx.type.replace(/_/g, " ")} · {new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                      <span className={tx.amount > 0 ? "text-emerald-400 font-semibold" : "text-red-400 font-semibold"}>
                        {tx.amount > 0 ? "+" : ""}{tx.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/public/HomePage.tsx

```tsx:src/pages/public/HomePage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { Play, Compass, Unlock, Crown, Clock, Sparkles, Wallet } from "lucide-react";
import PosterMosaic from "@/components/home/PosterMosaic";
import CategorySection from "@/components/home/CategorySection";
import FAQSection from "@/components/home/FAQSection";

import UnlockModal from "@/components/movie/UnlockModal";

import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { PremiumBadge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES, PREMIUM_PRICE } from "@/mocks/movies";
import { HOME_FEATURES, PREMIUM_BENEFITS, WALLET_BENEFITS } from "@/mocks/homeData";

const FEATURE_ICONS: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  Compass, Unlock, Crown, Play, Sparkles, Wallet,
};

export default function HomePage() {
  const [trailerOpen, setTrailerOpen] = useState(false);

  const { isAuthenticated, hasPremium, currentUser } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const featuredMovies = MOCK_MOVIES.filter((m) => m.isFeatured && m.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED");
  const watchHistory = isAuthenticated
    ? MOCK_WATCH_HISTORY.filter((h) => h.userId === currentUser?.id).slice(0, 8)
    : [];
  const recentlyUnlocked = isAuthenticated ? MOCK_MOVIES.filter((m) => m.accessMode === "LOCKED").slice(0, 6) : [];

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`
        @keyframes scrollLeft { 0% { transform: translateX(0); } 100% { transform: translateX(-33.333%); } }
        @keyframes scrollRight { 0% { transform: translateX(-33.333%); } 100% { transform: translateX(0); } }
        .scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
        .scrollbar-hide { -ms-overflow-style: none; scrollbar-width: none; }
      `}</style>

      {/* Hero with Poster Mosaic */}
      <div className="relative">
        <PosterMosaic onPlayTrailer={() => setTrailerOpen(true)} />

            Your Next Story<br />Starts Here

            Discover movies and series selected for every mood. Watch free content, unlock individual titles with your CineVault Wallet, or enjoy unlimited access with Premium.
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
            <Button onClick={() => navigate("/movies")} variant="primary" size="lg">
              Explore Movies
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={() => setTrailerOpen(true)} variant="secondary" size="lg">
              <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" /> Watch Trailer
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 pb-16">
        {/* Category section */}
        <CategorySection />

        {/* Auth-only sections */}
        {isAuthenticated && watchHistory.length > 0 && (
          <section className="py-8">
            <HorizontalScroll title="Continue Watching" subtitle="Pick up where you left off">
              {watchHistory.map((h) => {
                const movie = MOCK_MOVIES.find((m) => m.id === h.contentId);
                if (!movie) return null;
                return (
                  <MovieCard key={h.id} item={movie} type="movie" showProgress progressSeconds={h.progressSeconds} totalSeconds={h.totalSeconds} />
                );
              })}
            </HorizontalScroll>
          </section>
        )}

        {isAuthenticated && (
          <section className="py-8">
            <HorizontalScroll title="Recommended for You" subtitle="Based on your watch history">

                <MovieCard key={m.id} item={m} type="movie" onUnlock={(item) => setUnlockTarget({ movie: item as Movie, type: "movie" })} />
              ))}
            </HorizontalScroll>
          </section>

        {isAuthenticated && (
          <section className="py-8">
            <HorizontalScroll title="Recently Unlocked" subtitle="Content you've accessed">

                <MovieCard key={m.id} item={m} type="movie" />

              CineVault combines discovery, flexible access, personal recommendations, and seamless watch progress in one cinematic experience.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

              return (
                <div key={title} className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-card border border-border hover:border-primary/40 transition-all duration-200 group">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-primary/10 border border-primary/20 flex items-center justify-center mb-4 group-hover:bg-primary/20 transition-colors">
                    <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-foreground font-semibold mb-1.5">{title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm leading-relaxed">{desc}</p>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* FAQ */}
        <div id="faq">
          <FAQSection />

        {/* Access & Premium section */}
        <section className="py-12">

            {/* Wallet card */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-7 flex flex-col">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-2">Pay as You Watch</h3>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-4 leading-relaxed">
                Use your CineVault Wallet to unlock individual movies or episodes for their displayed access duration.
              </p>
              <ul className="space-y-2 mb-6 flex-1">
                {WALLET_BENEFITS.map((b) => (
                  <li key={b} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-emerald-900/40 border border-emerald-700/40 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                      <svg className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-emerald-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path fillRule="evenodd" d="M16.707 5.293a1 1 0 010 1.414l-8 8a1 1 0 01-1.414 0l-4-4a1 1 0 011.414-1.414L8 12.586l7.293-7.293a1 1 0 011.414 0z" clipRule="evenodd" /></svg>
                    </span>
                    {b}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black text-foreground mb-4">From 3,000 VND</p>
              <Button onClick={() => navigate("/movies")} variant="secondary" size="lg" fullWidth>

            </div>

            {/* Premium card */}

              <div className="absolute top-4 right-4"><PremiumBadge /></div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-2">CineVault Premium</h3>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-4 leading-relaxed">
                Access all published locked movies and episodes while your Premium subscription remains active.
              </p>
              <ul className="space-y-2 mb-6 flex-1">
                {PREMIUM_BENEFITS.map((b) => (
                  <li key={b} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-amber-900/40 border border-amber-700/40 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                      <Crown className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-amber-400" />
                    </span>
                    {b}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black text-foreground mb-1">{PREMIUM_PRICE.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND</p>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mb-4">per month · manual renewal</p>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <Button onClick={() => navigate("/subscription")} variant="primary" size="lg" fullWidth>
                  <Crown className="w-4 h-4" /> Get Premium

          contentType="MOVIE"
```

## src/pages/public/MovieDetailPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/public/MovieDetailPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { Play, Star, Clock, Heart, Share2, Lock, Check, Crown, ChevronLeft, Calendar, User } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { PremiumBadge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import TrailerModal from "@/components/movie/TrailerModal";
import UnlockModal from "@/components/movie/UnlockModal";
import MovieCard from "@/components/movie/MovieCard";
import { HorizontalScroll } from "@/components/movie/HorizontalScroll";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_MOVIES } from "@/mocks/movies";

export default function MovieDetailPage() {
  const { movieId } = useParams<{ movieId: string }>();
  const { canWatch, isAuthenticated, hasPremium, getAccess, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const [trailerOpen, setTrailerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [unlockOpen, setUnlockOpen] = useState(false);
  const [favorited, setFavorited] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const movie = MOCK_MOVIES.find((m) => m.id === movieId);
  if (!movie) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Movie not found</h1>
        <Link to="/movies" className="text-primary hover:underline">Browse all movies</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const access = getAccess(movie.id);
  const watchable = canWatch({ accessMode: movie.accessMode, publicationStatus: movie.publicationStatus, contentId: movie.id });
  const isLocked = movie.accessMode === "LOCKED" && !hasPremium && !access;
  const hasExpired = !access && movie.accessMode === "LOCKED";

  const similar = MOCK_MOVIES.filter((m) => m.id !== movie.id && m.genres.some((g) => movie.genres.includes(g))).slice(0, 8);

  function handleWatch() {
    if (!isAuthenticated) { navigate("/login"); return; }
    if (watchable) navigate(`/watch/movie/${movie.id}`);
    else setUnlockOpen(true);
  }

  return (
    <>
      {/* Backdrop */}
      <div className="relative w-full overflow-hidden" style={{ maxHeight: "520px" }}>
        <img src={movie.backdropUrl ?? movie.posterUrl} alt="" className="w-full h-[520px] object-cover" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background via-background/60 to-transparent" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-background/80 to-transparent" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 px-4 md:px-8 pb-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full">
          <button onClick={() => navigate(-1)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-sm mb-4 transition-colors">
            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Back
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 -mt-8 pb-16 relative z-10">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-8">
          {/* Poster */}
          <div className="flex-shrink-0">
            <div className="relative w-40 md:w-52 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-border shadow-2xl">
              <img src={movie.posterUrl} alt={movie.title} className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover" />
              {isLocked && (
                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/30">
                  <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Info */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-3 mb-2">
              {movie.genres.map((g) => (
                <span key={g} className="text-xs font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-muted border border-border text-muted-foreground">{g}</span>
              ))}
              {hasPremium && movie.accessMode === "LOCKED" && <PremiumBadge />}
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-foreground mb-1">{movie.title}</h1>
            {movie.originalTitle && movie.originalTitle !== movie.title && (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-3 italic">{movie.originalTitle}</p>
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Star className="w-4 h-4 text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400" />{movie.voteAverage.toFixed(1)}</span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Calendar className="w-4 h-4" />{movie.releaseYear}</span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Clock className="w-4 h-4" />{movie.runtimeMinutes} min</span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><User className="w-4 h-4" />{movie.director}</span>
            </div>

            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-2xl">{movie.overview}</p>

            {/* Access status */}
            {isLocked ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border mb-6 w-fit">
                <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-foreground font-semibold text-sm">
                    Unlock for {movie.resolvedPrice.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
                    Access for {movie.unlockDurationHours >= 24 ? `${movie.unlockDurationHours / 24} day${movie.unlockDurationHours / 24 !== 1 ? "s" : ""}` : `${movie.unlockDurationHours}h`}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : access ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900/20 border border-emerald-700/30 mb-6 w-fit">
                <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                <p className="text-emerald-300 text-sm font-medium">Unlocked until {new Date(access.expiresAt).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
              </div>
            ) : hasPremium && movie.accessMode === "LOCKED" ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/20 border border-amber-700/30 mb-6 w-fit">
                <Crown className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                <p className="text-amber-300 text-sm font-medium">Included with Premium</p>
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              <Button onClick={handleWatch} variant="primary" size="lg">
                <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" />
                {watchable ? "Watch Now" : isLocked ? `Unlock for ${movie.resolvedPrice.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND` : "Sign In to Watch"}
              </Button>
              {movie.trailerYoutubeId && (
                <Button onClick={() => setTrailerOpen(true)} variant="secondary" size="lg">
                  <Play className="w-4 h-4" /> Trailer
                </Button>
              )}
              <Button onClick={() => setFavorited((f) => !f)} variant="ghost" size="lg" className={favorited ? "text-red-400" : ""}>
                <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${favorited ? "fill-red-400 text-red-400" : ""}`} />
                {favorited ? "Saved" : "Favorite"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Cast */}
        {movie.cast.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mt-12">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Cast</h2>
            <div className="flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide pb-2">
              {movie.cast.map((c) => (
                <div key={c.name} className="flex-shrink-0 text-center" style={{ width: "clamp(80px, 10vw, 110px)" }}>
                  {c.photoUrl ? (
                    <img src={c.photoUrl} alt={c.name} className="w-full aspect-square object-cover rounded-full border-2 border-border mb-2" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full aspect-square rounded-full bg-muted border-2 border-border flex items-center justify-center mb-2">
                      <User className="w-6 h-6 text-muted-foreground" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <p className="text-foreground text-xs font-semibold truncate">{c.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs truncate">{c.role}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Similar */}
        {similar.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mt-12">
            <HorizontalScroll title="Similar Movies">
              {similar.map((m) => <MovieCard key={m.id} item={m} type="movie" onUnlock={() => setUnlockOpen(true)} />)}
            </HorizontalScroll>
          </section>
        )}
      </div>

      <TrailerModal open={trailerOpen} onClose={() => setTrailerOpen(false)} youtubeId={movie.trailerYoutubeId} title={movie.title} />
      {unlockOpen && (
        <UnlockModal open={true} onClose={() => setUnlockOpen(false)} contentId={movie.id} contentType="MOVIE" title={movie.title} price={movie.resolvedPrice} durationHours={movie.unlockDurationHours} />
      )}
      <style>{`.scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; } .scrollbar-hide { scrollbar-width: none; }`}</style>
    </>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/public/MoviesPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/public/MoviesPage.tsx
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router";
import { Search, SlidersHorizontal, X } from "lucide-react";
import MovieCard from "@/components/movie/MovieCard";
import UnlockModal from "@/components/movie/UnlockModal";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, ALL_GENRES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import type { Movie } from "@/types";

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Popularity", value: "popularity" },
  { label: "Rating", value: "rating" },
  { label: "Release Year", value: "year" },
  { label: "Title A–Z", value: "title" },
];

const YEARS = Array.from(new Set(MOCK_MOVIES.map((m) => m.releaseYear))).sort((a, b) => b - a);

export default function MoviesPage() {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(searchParams.get("q") ?? "");
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState(searchParams.get("genre") ?? "");
  const [year, setYear] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("popularity");
  const [access, setAccess] = useState("");
  const [filterOpen, setFilterOpen] = useState(false);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [unlockTarget, setUnlockTarget] = useState<Movie | null>(null);
  const PER_PAGE = 12;

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let list = MOCK_MOVIES.filter((m) => m.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED");
    if (search) list = list.filter((m) => m.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
    if (genre) list = list.filter((m) => m.genres.includes(genre));
    if (year) list = list.filter((m) => m.releaseYear === Number(year));
    if (access === "free") list = list.filter((m) => m.accessMode === "FREE");
    if (access === "locked") list = list.filter((m) => m.accessMode === "LOCKED");
    list = [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      if (sort === "popularity") return b.popularity - a.popularity;
      if (sort === "rating") return b.voteAverage - a.voteAverage;
      if (sort === "year") return b.releaseYear - a.releaseYear;
      return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
    });
    return list;
  }, [search, genre, year, sort, access]);

  const paginated = filtered.slice(0, page * PER_PAGE);
  const hasMore = paginated.length < filtered.length;

  function clearFilters() { setGenre(""); setYear(""); setAccess(""); setSearch(""); }
  const hasFilters = genre || year || access || search;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">Movies</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">{filtered.length} titles found</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setFilterOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card text-sm font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors md:hidden">
          <SlidersHorizontal className="w-4 h-4" /> Filters
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-6">
        {/* Filters sidebar / drawer */}
        <aside className={`flex-shrink-0 w-56 ${filterOpen ? "block fixed inset-y-0 right-0 z-50 bg-card border-l border-border p-6 overflow-y-auto" : "hidden md:block"}`}>
          {filterOpen && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
              <span className="font-semibold text-foreground">Filters</span>
              <button onClick={() => setFilterOpen(false)}><X className="w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" /></button>
            </div>
          )}
          {hasFilters && (
            <button onClick={clearFilters} className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline mb-4 block">Clear all filters</button>
          )}
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* Search */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Search</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <input value={search} onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }} placeholder="Title..." className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Genre */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Genre</label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {ALL_GENRES.map((g) => (
                  <button key={g} onClick={() => { setGenre(genre === g ? "" : g); setPage(1); }}
                    className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${genre === g ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
                    {g}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Year */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Year</label>
              <select value={year} onChange={(e) => { setYear(e.target.value); setPage(1); }} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50">
                <option value="">All Years</option>
                {YEARS.map((y) => <option key={y} value={y}>{y}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Access */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Access</label>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                {[{ label: "All", value: "" }, { label: "Free", value: "free" }, { label: "Locked", value: "locked" }].map((o) => (
                  <button key={o.value} onClick={() => { setAccess(o.value); setPage(1); }}
                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-left transition-colors ${access === o.value ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/20" : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted"}`}>
                    {o.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Sort */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Sort By</label>
              <select value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50">
                {SORT_OPTIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>
        {filterOpen && <div className="md:hidden fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-40" onClick={() => setFilterOpen(false)} />}

        {/* Grid */}
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          {filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-20 text-center gap-4">
              <Search className="w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground" />
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">No movies found</h3>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Try adjusting your filters or search term.</p>
              <button onClick={clearFilters} className="text-primary text-sm hover:underline">Clear filters</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
                {paginated.map((m) => (
                  <MovieCard key={m.id} item={m} type="movie" className="w-full" onUnlock={(item) => setUnlockTarget(item as Movie)} />
                ))}
              </div>
              {hasMore && (
                <div className="text-center mt-8">
                  <button onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)} className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card text-sm font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">
                    Load More
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {unlockTarget && (
        <UnlockModal open={true} onClose={() => setUnlockTarget(null)} contentId={unlockTarget.id} contentType="MOVIE" title={unlockTarget.title} price={unlockTarget.resolvedPrice} durationHours={unlockTarget.unlockDurationHours} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/public/SearchPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/public/SearchPage.tsx
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import MovieCard from "@/components/movie/MovieCard";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";

export default function SearchPage() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  const results = useMemo(() => {
    if (!query.trim()) return { movies: [], series: [] };
    const q = query.toLowerCase();
    return {
      movies: MOCK_MOVIES.filter((m) => m.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED" && m.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)),
      series: MOCK_SERIES.filter((s) => s.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED" && s.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)),
    };
  }, [query]);

  const total = results.movies.length + results.series.length;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <div className="max-w-xl mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Search</h1>
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" />
          <input
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            autoFocus
            placeholder="Search movies and series..."
            className="w-full pl-12 pr-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-card border border-border text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-base"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {query && (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-6">{total} result{total !== 1 ? "s" : ""} for "{query}"</p>
      )}

      {results.movies.length > 0 && (
        <section className="mb-10">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Movies</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
            {results.movies.map((m) => <MovieCard key={m.id} item={m} type="movie" className="w-full" />)}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {results.series.length > 0 && (
        <section>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-foreground mb-4">TV Series</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
            {results.series.map((s) => <MovieCard key={s.id} item={s} type="series" className="w-full" />)}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {query && total === 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-16 text-center gap-4">
          <Search className="w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground opacity-40" />
          <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">No results found</h3>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm max-w-xs">Try different keywords or browse our catalog.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {!query && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-16 text-center gap-3">
          <Search className="w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground opacity-20" />
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Start typing to search movies and series</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/public/SeriesDetailPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/public/SeriesDetailPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { Play, Star, Heart, Lock, Check, Crown, ChevronLeft, Calendar, User, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { PremiumBadge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import TrailerModal from "@/components/movie/TrailerModal";
import UnlockModal from "@/components/movie/UnlockModal";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import type { Episode } from "@/types";

export default function SeriesDetailPage() {
  const { seriesId } = useParams<{ seriesId: string }>();
  const { canWatch, isAuthenticated, hasPremium, getAccess } = useAuth();
  const [trailerOpen, setTrailerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSeason, setSelectedSeason] = useState(0);
  const [unlockEp, setUnlockEp] = useState<Episode | null>(null);
  const [favorited, setFavorited] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const series = MOCK_SERIES.find((s) => s.id === seriesId);
  if (!series) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Series not found</h1>
        <Link to="/series" className="text-primary hover:underline">Browse all series</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const season = series.seasons[selectedSeason];

  function handleEpWatch(ep: Episode) {
    if (!isAuthenticated) { navigate("/login"); return; }
    const watchable = canWatch({ accessMode: ep.accessMode, publicationStatus: ep.publicationStatus, contentId: ep.id });
    if (watchable) navigate(`/watch/episode/${ep.id}`);
    else setUnlockEp(ep);
  }

  return (
    <>
      {/* Backdrop */}
      <div className="relative w-full" style={{ maxHeight: "480px" }}>
        <img src={series.backdropUrl ?? series.posterUrl} alt="" className="w-full h-[480px] object-cover" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background via-background/60 to-transparent" />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-background/80 to-transparent" />
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 -mt-8 pb-16 relative z-10">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-8 mb-12">
          <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-40 md:w-52 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-border shadow-2xl">
            <img src={series.posterUrl} alt={series.title} className="w-full aspect-[2/3] object-cover" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-2">
              {series.genres.map((g) => <span key={g} className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-muted border border-border text-muted-foreground">{g}</span>)}
              {hasPremium && series.defaultAccessMode === "LOCKED" && <PremiumBadge />}
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-foreground mb-1">{series.title}</h1>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Star className="w-4 h-4 text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400" />{series.voteAverage.toFixed(1)}</span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Calendar className="w-4 h-4" />{series.releaseYear}</span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><User className="w-4 h-4" />{series.director}</span>
              <span>{series.seasons.length} Season{series.seasons.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-2xl">{series.overview}</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              {series.trailerYoutubeId && (
                <Button onClick={() => setTrailerOpen(true)} variant="secondary" size="lg">
                  <Play className="w-4 h-4" /> Watch Trailer
                </Button>
              )}
              <Button onClick={() => setFavorited((f) => !f)} variant="ghost" size="lg" className={favorited ? "text-red-400" : ""}>
                <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${favorited ? "fill-red-400 text-red-400" : ""}`} />
                {favorited ? "Saved" : "Favorite"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Season selector */}
        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-6 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide pb-1">
          {series.seasons.map((s, i) => (
            <button key={s.id} onClick={() => setSelectedSeason(i)}
              className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${i === selectedSeason ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
              {s.title}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Episode list */}
        {season && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {season.episodes.map((ep) => {
              const epAccess = getAccess(ep.id);
              const watchable = canWatch({ accessMode: ep.accessMode, publicationStatus: ep.publicationStatus, contentId: ep.id });
              const isLocked = ep.accessMode === "LOCKED" && !hasPremium && !epAccess;

              return (
                <div key={ep.id} className="flex gap-4 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/30 transition-colors">
                  {ep.thumbnailUrl && (
                    <div className="flex-shrink-0 relative w-32 aspect-video rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                      <img src={ep.thumbnailUrl} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                      {isLocked && (
                        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center">
                          <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mb-0.5">Episode {ep.episodeNumber}</p>
                        <p className="text-foreground font-semibold text-sm">{ep.title}</p>
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-1 line-clamp-2">{ep.overview}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{ep.durationMinutes} min</span>
                        {isLocked ? (
                          <button onClick={() => handleEpWatch(ep)} className="text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white font-semibold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors">
                            Unlock · {ep.resolvedPrice.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                          </button>
                        ) : epAccess ? (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-400">
                            <Check className="w-3 h-3" /> Unlocked
                          </div>
                        ) : hasPremium && ep.accessMode === "LOCKED" ? (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-amber-400">
                            <Crown className="w-3 h-3" /> Premium
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <button onClick={() => handleEpWatch(ep)} className="text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-muted border border-border text-foreground font-semibold hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors">
                            <Play className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1" /> Watch
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <TrailerModal open={trailerOpen} onClose={() => setTrailerOpen(false)} youtubeId={series.trailerYoutubeId} title={series.title} />
      {unlockEp && (
        <UnlockModal open={true} onClose={() => setUnlockEp(null)} contentId={unlockEp.id} contentType="EPISODE" title={unlockEp.title} price={unlockEp.resolvedPrice} durationHours={unlockEp.unlockDurationHours} />
      )}
      <style>{`.scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; } .scrollbar-hide { scrollbar-width: none; }`}</style>
    </>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/public/SeriesPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/public/SeriesPage.tsx
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Search, SlidersHorizontal, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { Star, Lock } from "lucide-react";
import { MOCK_SERIES, ALL_GENRES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Popularity", value: "popularity" },
  { label: "Rating", value: "rating" },
  { label: "Release Year", value: "year" },
  { label: "Title A–Z", value: "title" },
];

export default function SeriesPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("popularity");
  const [access, setAccess] = useState("");
  const [filterOpen, setFilterOpen] = useState(false);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const { hasPremium } = useAuth();
  const PER_PAGE = 12;

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let list = MOCK_SERIES.filter((s) => s.publicationStatus === "PUBLISHED");
    if (search) list = list.filter((s) => s.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
    if (genre) list = list.filter((s) => s.genres.includes(genre));
    if (access === "free") list = list.filter((s) => s.defaultAccessMode === "FREE");
    if (access === "locked") list = list.filter((s) => s.defaultAccessMode === "LOCKED");
    list = [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      if (sort === "popularity") return b.popularity - a.popularity;
      if (sort === "rating") return b.voteAverage - a.voteAverage;
      if (sort === "year") return b.releaseYear - a.releaseYear;
      return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
    });
    return list;
  }, [search, genre, sort, access]);

  const paginated = filtered.slice(0, page * PER_PAGE);
  const hasMore = paginated.length < filtered.length;
  const hasFilters = genre || access || search;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">TV Series</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">{filtered.length} series found</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setFilterOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card text-sm font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors md:hidden">
          <SlidersHorizontal className="w-4 h-4" /> Filters
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-6">
        <aside className={`flex-shrink-0 w-56 ${filterOpen ? "block fixed inset-y-0 right-0 z-50 bg-card border-l border-border p-6 overflow-y-auto" : "hidden md:block"}`}>
          {filterOpen && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
              <span className="font-semibold">Filters</span>
              <button onClick={() => setFilterOpen(false)}><X className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
            </div>
          )}
          {hasFilters && <button onClick={() => { setGenre(""); setAccess(""); setSearch(""); }} className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline mb-4 block">Clear all</button>}
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Search</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <input value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Series title..." className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Genre</label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {ALL_GENRES.map((g) => (
                  <button key={g} onClick={() => setGenre(genre === g ? "" : g)} className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${genre === g ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>{g}</button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Access</label>
              {[{ label: "All", value: "" }, { label: "Free", value: "free" }, { label: "Locked", value: "locked" }].map((o) => (
                <button key={o.value} onClick={() => setAccess(o.value)} className={`block w-full text-left px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium mb-1 transition-colors ${access === o.value ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/20" : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted"}`}>{o.label}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">Sort By</label>
              <select value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
                {SORT_OPTIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>
        {filterOpen && <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-40 md:hidden" onClick={() => setFilterOpen(false)} />}

        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          {filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-20 text-center gap-4">
              <Search className="w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground" />
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">No series found</h3>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Try adjusting your filters.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
                {paginated.map((s) => {
                  const isLocked = s.defaultAccessMode === "LOCKED" && !hasPremium;
                  return (
                    <Link key={s.id} to={`/series/${s.id}`} className="group relative">
                      <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border">
                        <img src={s.posterUrl} alt={s.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-110" />
                        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/0 group-hover:bg-black/40 transition-all duration-300" />
                        {isLocked && (
                          <div className="absolute top-2 left-2">
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-black/60 text-white">
                              <Lock className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> Locked
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                        {s.defaultAccessMode === "FREE" && (
                          <div className="absolute top-2 left-2">
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600/90 text-white">FREE</span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 flex items-center gap-1 bg-black/60 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5">
                          <Star className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400" />
                          <span className="text-white text-[10px] font-semibold">{s.voteAverage.toFixed(1)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="absolute bottom-2 left-2 right-2">
                          <span className="text-[10px] bg-black/60 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-medium">
                            {s.seasons.length} Season{s.seasons.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="mt-2 px-0.5">
                        <p className="text-foreground text-sm font-medium truncate">{s.title}</p>
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{s.releaseYear} · {s.genres[0]}</p>
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              {hasMore && (
                <div className="text-center mt-8">
                  <button onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)} className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card text-sm font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors">Load More</button>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/user/FavoritesPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/user/FavoritesPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Heart, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_FAVORITES } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";

export default function FavoritesPage() {
  const { currentUser, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const [removed, setRemoved] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const favorites = isAuthenticated
    ? MOCK_FAVORITES.filter((f) => f.userId === currentUser!.id && !removed.includes(f.id))
    : [];

  function getPoster(fav: typeof favorites[0]) {
    if (fav.contentType === "MOVIE") return MOCK_MOVIES.find((m) => m.id === fav.contentId)?.posterUrl ?? "";
    return MOCK_SERIES.find((s) => s.id === fav.contentId)?.posterUrl ?? "";
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Sign in to view your Favorites</h1>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground mb-8 flex items-center gap-2">
        <Heart className="w-6 h-6 text-red-400 fill-red-400" /> My Favorites
      </h1>

      {favorites.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-20 text-center gap-4">
          <Heart className="w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground opacity-20" />
          <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">No favorites yet</h3>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Browse movies and series and save what you love.</p>
          <Link to="/movies" className="text-primary hover:underline text-sm">Explore Movies</Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
          {favorites.map((fav) => {
            const href = fav.contentType === "MOVIE" ? `/movie/${fav.contentId}` : `/series/${fav.contentId}`;
            const poster = getPoster(fav);
            return (
              <div key={fav.id} className="relative group">
                <Link to={href}>
                  <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border">
                    {poster ? (
                      <img src={poster} alt={fav.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-110" />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="w-full h-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center">
                        <Heart className="w-8 h-8 text-muted-foreground" />
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/0 group-hover:bg-black/30 transition-all duration-300" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-foreground text-sm font-medium mt-2 truncate px-0.5">{fav.title}</p>
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs px-0.5">{fav.contentType === "MOVIE" ? "Movie" : "Series"}</p>
                </Link>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setRemoved((r) => [...r, fav.id])}
                  className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-black/70 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity hover:bg-red-900/80"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white" />
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## src/pages/user/HistoryPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/user/HistoryPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { History, Trash2, Play, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_WATCH_HISTORY } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";

export default function HistoryPage() {
  const { currentUser, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const [removed, setRemoved] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [clearOpen, setClearOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cleared, setCleared] = useState(false);

  const history = isAuthenticated && !cleared
    ? MOCK_WATCH_HISTORY.filter((h) => h.userId === currentUser!.id && !removed.includes(h.id))
    : [];

  function getPoster(h: typeof history[0]) {
    if (h.contentType === "MOVIE") return MOCK_MOVIES.find((m) => m.id === h.contentId)?.posterUrl ?? "";
    return MOCK_SERIES.find((s) => s.seasons.some((season) => season.episodes.some((ep) => ep.id === h.contentId)))?.posterUrl ?? "";
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center"><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Sign in to view your Watch History</h1></div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
          <History className="w-6 h-6" /> Watch History
        </h1>
        {history.length > 0 && (
          <button onClick={() => setClearOpen(true)} className="text-sm text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors">
            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Clear History
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {history.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-20 text-center gap-4">
          <History className="w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground opacity-20" />
          <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">No watch history</h3>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Start watching to see your history here.</p>
          <Link to="/movies" className="text-primary hover:underline text-sm">Browse Movies</Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {history.map((h) => {
            const poster = getPoster(h);
            const progressPct = h.totalSeconds > 0 ? Math.min(100, (h.progressSeconds / h.totalSeconds) * 100) : 0;
            const remaining = Math.round((h.totalSeconds - h.progressSeconds) / 60);
            const watchHref = h.contentType === "MOVIE" ? `/watch/movie/${h.contentId}` : `/watch/episode/${h.contentId}`;

            return (
              <div key={h.id} className="group flex gap-4 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/30 transition-colors">
                <div className="flex-shrink-0 relative w-28 aspect-video rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                  {poster ? (
                    <img src={poster} alt={h.contentTitle} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center">
                      <Play className="w-6 h-6 text-muted-foreground" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 h-1 bg-black/40">
                    <div className="h-full bg-primary" style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }} />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-foreground font-semibold text-sm mb-0.5 truncate">{h.contentTitle}</p>
                  {h.episodeInfo && (
                    <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mb-1">{h.episodeInfo.seriesTitle} · S{h.episodeInfo.season}E{h.episodeInfo.episode}</p>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>{Math.round(progressPct)}% watched</span>
                    {remaining > 0 && <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5"><Clock className="w-3 h-3" />{remaining}m remaining</span>}
                    <span>{new Date(h.lastWatchedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <Link to={watchHref} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors">
                    <Play className="w-3 h-3 fill-white" /> Continue
                  </Link>
                </div>

                <button onClick={() => setRemoved((r) => [...r, h.id])} className="self-start w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity hover:bg-muted/70 flex-shrink-0">
                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={clearOpen}
        onClose={() => setClearOpen(false)}
        onConfirm={() => { setCleared(true); setClearOpen(false); }}
        title="Clear Watch History"
        description="Are you sure you want to clear your entire watch history? This action cannot be undone."
        confirmLabel="Clear All"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/user/ProfilePage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/user/ProfilePage.tsx
import { Crown, User, Mail, Shield, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { PremiumBadge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import { Link } from "react-router";

export default function ProfilePage() {
  const { currentUser, isAuthenticated, hasPremium } = useAuth();

  if (!isAuthenticated || !currentUser) {
    return <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center"><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Sign in to view your Profile</h1></div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground mb-8">My Profile</h1>

      {/* Avatar card */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-8 mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-primary/20 border-2 border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
          <span className="text-3xl font-black text-primary">{currentUser.name[0]}</span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-1">{currentUser.name}</h2>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-3">{currentUser.email}</p>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {hasPremium && <PremiumBadge />}
          <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold ${currentUser.accountStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-700/30" : "bg-red-900/40 text-red-400 border border-red-700/30"}`}>
            {currentUser.accountStatus}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Info grid */}
      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-6">
        {[
          { icon: User, label: "Full Name", value: currentUser.name },
          { icon: Mail, label: "Email", value: currentUser.email },
          { icon: Shield, label: "Role", value: currentUser.role },
          { icon: Clock, label: "Member Since", value: new Date(currentUser.createdAt).toLocaleDateString() },
        ].map(({ icon: Icon, label, value }) => (
          <div key={label} className="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <Icon className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-foreground font-medium text-sm">{value}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Quick links */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-4">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground mb-3">Quick Links</h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          {[
            { label: "My Wallet", href: "/wallet" },
            { label: "Favorites", href: "/favorites" },
            { label: "Watch History", href: "/history" },
            { label: "Premium", href: "/subscription" },
          ].map(({ label, href }) => (
            <Link key={href} to={href} className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-muted text-sm font-medium text-foreground hover:bg-muted/70 transition-colors text-center">{label}</Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
