## src/pages/user/SubscriptionPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/user/SubscriptionPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Crown, Check, Clock, AlertTriangle, Wallet } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { PREMIUM_PRICE } from "@/mocks/movies";
import { PREMIUM_BENEFITS } from "@/mocks/homeData";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router";

export default function SubscriptionPage() {
  const { currentUser, hasPremium, isAuthenticated, addFunds } = useAuth();
  const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const balance = currentUser?.walletBalance ?? 0;
  const insufficient = balance < PREMIUM_PRICE;

  function handleSubscribe() {
    if (!isAuthenticated) { navigate("/login"); return; }
    setConfirmOpen(true);
  }

  function confirmSubscribe() {
    setProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.success("CineVault Premium activated! Enjoy unlimited access.");
      setConfirmOpen(false);
      setProcessing(false);
    }, 1000);
  }

  const subStatus = currentUser?.premium.status;
  const expDate = currentUser?.premium.expirationDate;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <div className="text-center mb-12">
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-foreground mb-3">CineVault Premium</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-base max-w-lg mx-auto">
          Access all published locked movies and episodes while your Premium subscription remains active.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Status card for logged-in users */}
      {isAuthenticated && subStatus && subStatus !== "NONE" && (
        <div className={`rounded-2xl border p-6 mb-8 flex items-start gap-4 ${subStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "border-amber-700/40 bg-amber-950/20" : "border-red-700/30 bg-red-950/20"}`}>
          <Crown className={`w-6 h-6 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${subStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "text-amber-400" : "text-red-400"}`} />
          <div>
            <p className={`font-semibold ${subStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "text-amber-300" : "text-red-300"}`}>
              {subStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "Premium Active" : "Premium Expired"}
            </p>
            {expDate && (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                <Clock className="w-3 h-3" />
                {subStatus === "ACTIVE" ? "Expires" : "Expired"} {new Date(expDate).toLocaleDateString()}
              </p>
            )}
            {subStatus === "ACTIVE" && (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-1">Manual renewal required — no automatic charges.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Premium card */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-700/40 bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-950/30 to-card p-8 md:p-10 relative overflow-hidden mx-auto max-w-md">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 rounded-full bg-amber-500/5 -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2" />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/40 border border-amber-700/30 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Crown className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground">CineVault Premium</h2>
            <p className="text-amber-400 text-xs font-semibold">ONE PLAN · MANUAL RENEWAL</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-6">
          <span className="text-4xl font-black text-foreground">{PREMIUM_PRICE.toLocaleString("vi-VN")}</span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-1">VND / month</span>
        </div>

        <ul className="space-y-3 mb-8">
          {PREMIUM_BENEFITS.map((b) => (
            <li key={b} className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-900/40 border border-amber-700/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
              </span>
              {b}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>

        {isAuthenticated && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-muted/50 border border-border mb-4 text-sm">
            <Wallet className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">Wallet:</span>
            <span className={`font-semibold ${insufficient ? "text-red-400" : "text-foreground"}`}>
              {balance.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
            </span>
            {insufficient && <span className="text-red-400 text-xs ml-auto">Insufficient</span>}
          </div>
        )}

        <Button
          fullWidth
          size="lg"
          variant={hasPremium ? "secondary" : "primary"}
          onClick={handleSubscribe}
        >
          <Crown className="w-4 h-4" />
          {!isAuthenticated ? "Sign In to Subscribe" : hasPremium ? "Renew Premium" : "Get Premium"}
        </Button>

        {isAuthenticated && insufficient && (
          <Button fullWidth size="md" variant="ghost" className="mt-2" onClick={() => navigate("/wallet")}>
            Add Funds to Wallet
          </Button>
        )}

        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs text-center mt-4">
          No automatic renewal · Paid via CineVault Wallet · Non-refundable
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Confirm dialog */}
      <Modal open={confirmOpen} onClose={() => setConfirmOpen(false)} title="Confirm Premium Subscription" size="sm">
        {insufficient ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 text-center">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-10 h-10 text-amber-400" />
            <p className="text-foreground font-semibold">Insufficient wallet balance</p>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">You need {PREMIUM_PRICE.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND but only have {balance.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND.</p>
            <Button fullWidth onClick={() => { setConfirmOpen(false); navigate("/wallet"); }}>Add Funds</Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-4">
              You are about to subscribe to <strong className="text-foreground">CineVault Premium</strong> for {PREMIUM_PRICE.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND.
              Access will remain active for 1 month. This payment is non-refundable and will be deducted from your wallet.
            </p>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-4">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">Amount</span>
              <span className="text-foreground font-semibold">{PREMIUM_PRICE.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm border-t border-border pt-3 mb-6">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">Remaining balance</span>
              <span className="text-foreground font-semibold">{(balance - PREMIUM_PRICE).toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <Button fullWidth variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirmOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button fullWidth variant="primary" loading={processing} onClick={confirmSubscribe}>
                <Crown className="w-4 h-4" /> Confirm
              </Button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/user/WalletPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/user/WalletPage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Plus, ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownLeft, TrendingUp, Receipt, Info } from "lucide-react";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_TRANSACTIONS } from "@/mocks/transactions";
import type { WalletTransaction } from "@/types";
import { toast } from "sonner";

const TOP_UP_AMOUNTS = [20000, 50000, 100000];

const TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  TOP_UP: "Top Up",
  MOVIE_UNLOCK: "Movie Unlock",
  EPISODE_UNLOCK: "Episode Unlock",
  SUBSCRIPTION_PAYMENT: "Premium Payment",
  ADMIN_ADJUSTMENT: "Admin Adjustment",
};

export default function WalletPage() {
  const { currentUser, addFunds, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const [addOpen, setAddOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedAmount, setSelectedAmount] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [customAmount, setCustomAmount] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("");
  const [detailTx, setDetailTx] = useState<WalletTransaction | null>(null);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);

  const balance = currentUser?.walletBalance ?? 0;
  const transactions = currentUser
    ? MOCK_TRANSACTIONS.filter((t) => t.userId === currentUser.id)
    : [];
  const filtered = filter ? transactions.filter((t) => t.type === filter) : transactions;

  function handleAddFunds() {
    const amt = selectedAmount ?? (customAmount ? Number(customAmount) : 0);
    if (!amt || amt <= 0) { toast.error("Enter a valid amount"); return; }
    setProcessing(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      addFunds(amt);
      toast.success(`${amt.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND added to your wallet!`);
      setAddOpen(false);
      setSelectedAmount(null);
      setCustomAmount("");
      setProcessing(false);
    }, 800);
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground mb-4">Sign in to view your Wallet</h1>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-foreground mb-8">My Wallet</h1>

      {/* Balance card */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-gradient-to-br from-card to-muted/30 p-8 mb-8 relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-40 h-40 rounded-full bg-primary/5 -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2" />
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-1">Current Balance</p>
        <p className="text-4xl font-black text-foreground mb-4">{balance.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} <span className="text-xl text-muted-foreground font-medium">VND</span></p>
        <Button onClick={() => setAddOpen(true)} variant="primary" size="lg">
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Add Funds
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* Transactions */}
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4 flex-wrap gap-2">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-foreground">Transaction History</h2>
          <select value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none">
            <option value="">All Types</option>
            {Object.entries(TYPE_LABELS).map(([v, l]) => <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>

        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-16 text-center gap-3">
            <Receipt className="w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground opacity-30" />
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">No transactions yet</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {filtered.map((tx) => {
              const isCredit = tx.amount > 0;
              return (
                <button key={tx.id} onClick={() => setDetailTx(tx)} className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/30 transition-colors text-left">
                  <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 ${isCredit ? "bg-emerald-900/40 border border-emerald-700/30" : "bg-red-900/20 border border-red-700/20"}`}>
                    {isCredit ? <ArrowDownLeft className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" /> : <ArrowUpRight className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400" />}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-foreground font-semibold text-sm">{TYPE_LABELS[tx.type] ?? tx.type}</p>
                    {tx.relatedContentTitle && <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs truncate">{tx.relatedContentTitle}</p>}
                    <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
                    <p className={`font-bold text-sm ${isCredit ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-red-400"}`}>
                      {isCredit ? "+" : ""}{tx.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{tx.status}</p>
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Add Funds modal */}
      <Modal open={addOpen} onClose={() => setAddOpen(false)} title="Add Funds" size="sm">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-4">Select an amount or enter a custom value.</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-4">
          {TOP_UP_AMOUNTS.map((amt) => (
            <button key={amt} onClick={() => { setSelectedAmount(amt); setCustomAmount(""); }}
              className={`py-3 rounded-xl border text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${selectedAmount === amt ? "bg-primary text-white border-primary" : "bg-muted border-border text-foreground hover:border-primary/50"}`}>
              {(amt / 1000).toFixed(0)}K VND
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="mb-6">
          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground block mb-2">Custom amount (VND)</label>
          <input type="number" value={customAmount} onChange={(e) => { setCustomAmount(e.target.value); setSelectedAmount(null); }} placeholder="e.g. 75000" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-muted border border-border text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:border-primary/50 text-sm" />
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground italic mb-4">This is a mock payment — no real charge is made.</p>
        <Button fullWidth onClick={handleAddFunds} loading={processing} disabled={!selectedAmount && !customAmount}>
          Confirm Top Up
        </Button>
      </Modal>

      {/* Transaction detail modal */}
      <Modal open={!!detailTx} onClose={() => setDetailTx(null)} title="Transaction Details" size="sm">
        {detailTx && (
          <div className="space-y-3 text-sm">
            {[
              ["ID", detailTx.id],
              ["Type", TYPE_LABELS[detailTx.type]],
              ["Status", detailTx.status],
              ["Amount", `${detailTx.amount > 0 ? "+" : ""}${detailTx.amount.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
              ["Balance Before", `${detailTx.balanceBefore.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
              ["Balance After", `${detailTx.balanceAfter.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND`],
              detailTx.relatedContentTitle ? ["Content", detailTx.relatedContentTitle] : null,
              detailTx.purchasedDurationHours ? ["Duration", `${detailTx.purchasedDurationHours}h`] : null,
              detailTx.reason ? ["Reason", detailTx.reason] : null,
              ["Date", new Date(detailTx.createdAt).toLocaleString()],
            ].filter(Boolean).map(([label, value]) => (
              <div key={label} className="flex justify-between gap-4">
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>
                <span className="text-foreground font-medium text-right">{value}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/pages/user/WatchPage.tsx
```tsx:src/pages/user/WatchPage.tsx
import { useParams, useNavigate, Link } from "react-router";
import { ChevronLeft, Lock, Play, Crown, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { MOCK_MOVIES, MOCK_SERIES } from "@/mocks/movies";
import type { Movie, Episode } from "@/types";

function findEpisode(episodeId: string) {
  for (const series of MOCK_SERIES) {
    for (const season of series.seasons) {
      const ep = season.episodes.find((e) => e.id === episodeId);
      if (ep) return { ep, series };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export default function WatchPage() {
  const { movieId, episodeId } = useParams();
  const { canWatch, hasPremium, getAccess } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  let content: Movie | Episode | null = null;
  let title = "";
  let contentType: "MOVIE" | "EPISODE" = "MOVIE";
  let accessMode: "FREE" | "LOCKED" = "FREE";
  let resolvedPrice = 0;
  let durationHours = 0;
  let backHref = "/movies";

  if (movieId) {
    content = MOCK_MOVIES.find((m) => m.id === movieId) ?? null;
    if (content) {
      const m = content as Movie;
      title = m.title;
      accessMode = m.accessMode;
      resolvedPrice = m.resolvedPrice;
      durationHours = m.unlockDurationHours;
      backHref = `/movie/${movieId}`;
    }
  } else if (episodeId) {
    const found = findEpisode(episodeId);
    if (found) {
      content = found.ep;
      const ep = found.ep;
      title = `${found.series.title} – ${ep.title}`;
      contentType = "EPISODE";
      accessMode = ep.accessMode;
      resolvedPrice = ep.resolvedPrice;
      durationHours = ep.unlockDurationHours;
      backHref = `/series/${found.series.id}`;
    }
  }

  if (!content) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Content not found.</p>
        <Link to="/" className="text-primary hover:underline mt-4 block">Go Home</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const access = getAccess(content.id);
  const watchable = canWatch({ accessMode, publicationStatus: "PUBLISHED", contentId: content.id });

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8">
      <button onClick={() => navigate(backHref)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-sm mb-6 transition-colors">
        <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Back to details
      </button>

      {watchable ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-border bg-black aspect-video flex items-center justify-center relative">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-white/10 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Play className="w-10 h-10 text-white fill-white ml-1" />
            </div>
            <p className="text-white font-semibold text-lg">{title}</p>
            <p className="text-white/50 text-sm">Video playback placeholder</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card aspect-video flex items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-center flex flex-col items-center gap-6 max-w-sm px-4">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-muted border border-border flex items-center justify-center">
              <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-primary" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-2">{title}</h2>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mb-2">This content is locked.</p>
              <p className="text-foreground font-semibold">{resolvedPrice.toLocaleString("vi-VN")} VND</p>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
                Access for {durationHours >= 24 ? `${durationHours / 24} day${durationHours / 24 !== 1 ? "s" : ""}` : `${durationHours}h`}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 w-full">
              <Button onClick={() => navigate(backHref)} variant="primary" fullWidth>
                Unlock Content
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={() => navigate("/subscription")} variant="ghost" fullWidth>
                <Crown className="w-4 h-4" /> Or get Premium
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={() => navigate("/wallet")} variant="outline" fullWidth>
                <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Add Wallet Funds
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-4 p-4 rounded-xl bg-card border border-border">
        <h3 className="text-foreground font-semibold mb-1">{title}</h3>
        {access && (
          <p className="text-emerald-400 text-sm">Unlocked until {new Date(access.expiresAt).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
        )}
        {hasPremium && accessMode === "LOCKED" && !access && (
          <p className="text-amber-400 text-sm flex items-center gap-1"><Crown className="w-3 h-3" /> Included with Premium</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## src/types/index.ts
```typescript:src/types/index.ts
// ── User ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export type UserRole = "USER" | "ADMIN";
export type AccountStatus = "ACTIVE" | "LOCKED";
export type PremiumStatus = "ACTIVE" | "EXPIRED" | "NONE";

export interface User {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  avatar?: string;
  role: UserRole;
  accountStatus: AccountStatus;
  walletBalance: number; // VND
  premium: {
    status: PremiumStatus;
    startDate?: string;
    expirationDate?: string;
  };
  createdAt: string;
}

// ── Content Access ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// ── Wallet & Transactions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
```

## vite.figma.config.ts
```typescript:vite.figma.config.ts
import {
  defineConfig,
  mergeConfig,
  type ConfigEnv,
  type HtmlTagDescriptor,
  type Plugin,
  type UserConfig,
} from 'vite'
import path from 'node:path'
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'

import siteConfiguration from './.figma/make/site.json'
import packageAliases from './.figma/make/migrated-package-aliases.json'
import userConfig from './vite.config'

const FIGMA_ASSET_PREFIX = 'figma:asset/'
const ASSET_FILENAME_ALIASES_PATH = '.figma/make/asset-filename-aliases.json'
const SOURCE_PATH_ALIASES_PATH = '.figma/make/source-path-aliases.json'

function loadAssetFilenameAliases(): Record<string, string> {
  let contents: string
  try {
    contents = readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, ASSET_FILENAME_ALIASES_PATH), 'utf8')
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') return {}
    throw error
  }

  const aliases: unknown = JSON.parse(contents)
  if (aliases === null || typeof aliases !== 'object' || Array.isArray(aliases)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid asset filename aliases at ${ASSET_FILENAME_ALIASES_PATH}`)
  }
  for (const [original, stored] of Object.entries(aliases)) {
    if (
      !original ||
      typeof stored !== 'string' ||
      !stored ||
      stored === '.' ||
      stored === '..' ||
      stored !== path.basename(stored) ||
      stored.includes('\\') ||
      stored.includes('\0')
    ) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid asset filename alias for ${original}`)
    }
  }
  return aliases as Record<string, string>
}

export default defineConfig(async (env) =>
  mergeConfig(await resolveUserConfig(env), {
    resolve: {
      alias: Object.entries<string>(packageAliases).map(([find, replacement]) => ({
        find,
        replacement,
      })),
    },
    base: process.env.FIGMA_PUBLIC_URL ? `${process.env.FIGMA_PUBLIC_URL}/` : '/',
    build: {
      sourcemap: env.mode === 'development' ? 'inline' : false,
      minify: env.mode !== 'development',
    },
    plugins: [
      figmaSourcePathResolver(),
      figmaPackageSchemeResolver(),
      figmaAssetResolver(),
      figmaSiteConfiguration(siteConfiguration),
      figmaErrorOverlayReplay(),
      figmaReactRefreshBoundaryFallback(),
      figmaMakeKitPlugin({ storiesGlob: '/src/**/*.stories.{ts,tsx,js,jsx}' }),
    ],
    server: {
      host: '0.0.0.0',
      port: parseInt(process.env.PORT || '8443'),
      strictPort: true,
      watch: { ignored: ['**/.figma/**'] },
    },
    preview: {
      host: '0.0.0.0',
      port: parseInt(process.env.PORT || '8443'),
    },
  }),
)

async function resolveUserConfig(env: ConfigEnv): Promise<UserConfig> {
  return typeof userConfig === 'function' ? await userConfig(env) : await userConfig
}

function isProjectPath(value: unknown): value is string {
  return (
    typeof value === 'string' &&
    value.length > 0 &&
    !value.includes('\\') &&
    !value.includes('\0') &&
    !path.isAbsolute(value) &&
    !path.posix.isAbsolute(value) &&
    value.split('/').every((part) => part !== '' && part !== '.' && part !== '..')
  )
}

function projectPath(root: string, absolute: string): string | undefined {
  const relative = path.relative(root, path.resolve(absolute)).split(path.sep).join('/')
  if (relative === '.figma' || relative.startsWith('.figma/')) return
  return isProjectPath(relative) ? relative : undefined
}

function loadSourcePathAliases(root: string): Map<string, string> {
  let contents: string
  try {
    contents = readFileSync(path.join(root, SOURCE_PATH_ALIASES_PATH), 'utf8')
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') return new Map()
    throw error
  }

  const aliases: unknown = JSON.parse(contents)
  if (aliases === null || typeof aliases !== 'object' || Array.isArray(aliases)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid source path aliases at ${SOURCE_PATH_ALIASES_PATH}`)
  }
  const result = new Map<string, string>()
  for (const [original, stored] of Object.entries(aliases)) {
    if (
      !isProjectPath(original) ||
      !isProjectPath(stored) ||
      /[?#]/.test(original) ||
      /[?#]/.test(stored) ||
      path.posix.dirname(original) !== path.posix.dirname(stored) ||
      projectPath(root, path.resolve(root, original)) !== original ||
      projectPath(root, path.resolve(root, stored)) !== stored
    ) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid source path alias for ${original}`)
    }
    result.set(original, stored)
  }
  return result
}

function figmaSourcePathResolver(): Plugin {
  const root = path.resolve(__dirname)
  const aliases = loadSourcePathAliases(root)
  return {
    name: 'figma-source-path-resolver',
    enforce: 'pre',
    resolveId(id, importer) {
      if (aliases.size === 0 || id.includes('\0') || id.includes('\\') || /[?#]/.test(id)) return
      const importerPath = importer ? projectPath(root, importer.split(/[?#]/)[0]!) : undefined
      if (importer && !importerPath) return

      const importedPath = (value: string): string | undefined => {
        if (value.startsWith('/@fs/')) return projectPath(root, value.slice('/@fs'.length))
        // Vite's alias plugin resolves configured aliases, including @, before this hook.
        if (value.startsWith(`${root}/`)) return projectPath(root, value)
        if (value.startsWith('/')) return projectPath(root, path.resolve(root, `.${value}`))
        if (
          importerPath &&
          (value === '.' || value === '..' || value.startsWith('./') || value.startsWith('../'))
        ) {
          return projectPath(root, path.resolve(root, path.dirname(importerPath), value))
        }
      }

      const original = importedPath(id)
      const stored = original && aliases.get(original)
      if (stored) return path.resolve(root, stored)
    },
  }
}

function figmaPackageSchemeResolver(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'figma-package-scheme-resolver',
    enforce: 'pre',
    async resolveId(id, importer) {
      let normalizedId: string | undefined
      if (id.startsWith('npm:')) {
        normalizedId = id.slice(4)
      } else if (id.startsWith('jsr:@')) {
        normalizedId = `@jsr/${id.slice(5).replace('/', '__')}`
      }

      if (!normalizedId) return
      return this.resolve(normalizedId, importer, { skipSelf: true })
    },
  }
}

function figmaAssetResolver(): Plugin {
  const filenameAliases = loadAssetFilenameAliases()
  return {
    name: 'figma-asset-resolver',
    resolveId(id) {
      if (!id.startsWith(FIGMA_ASSET_PREFIX)) return
      const { filename, postfix } = resolveAssetFilename(
        filenameAliases,
        id.slice(FIGMA_ASSET_PREFIX.length),
      )
      return path.resolve(__dirname, 'assets', path.basename(filename)) + postfix
    },
  }
}

function resolveAssetFilename(
  aliases: Record<string, string>,
  importedFilename: string,
): { filename: string; postfix: string } {
  // An exact alias can include literal query or fragment characters in its original name.
  const exactBasename = path.basename(importedFilename)
  const possiblePostfixStart = importedFilename.search(/[?#]/)
  const exactAlias =
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(aliases, exactBasename) &&
    (possiblePostfixStart < 0 || !importedFilename.slice(possiblePostfixStart).includes('/'))
  const postfixStart = exactAlias ? -1 : possiblePostfixStart
  const filename = postfixStart < 0 ? importedFilename : importedFilename.slice(0, postfixStart)
  let postfix = postfixStart < 0 ? '' : importedFilename.slice(postfixStart)
  const basename = path.basename(filename)
  const aliased = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(aliases, basename)
  const storedFilename = aliased ? aliases[basename]! : filename

  // Vite otherwise parses extensionless images as JavaScript. Explicit raw imports
  // still export the file contents, and existing queries and fragments stay intact.
  if (aliased && !path.extname(storedFilename)) {
    const hashStart = postfix.indexOf('#')
    let query = hashStart < 0 ? postfix : postfix.slice(0, hashStart)
    const fragment = hashStart < 0 ? '' : postfix.slice(hashStart)
    if (!/[?&](?:raw|url)(?:&|$)/.test(query)) {
      query += `${query ? '&' : '?'}url`
    }
    // Vite requires an ampersand after a raw/url flag followed by a fragment.
    postfix = query + (fragment && /[?&](?:raw|url)$/.test(query) ? '&' : '') + fragment
  }
  return { filename: storedFilename, postfix }
}

type FigmaSiteConfiguration = {
  title?: string
  description?: string
  language?: string
  robots?: { index?: boolean }
  icons?: { icon?: string }
  openGraph?: { image?: string }
  analytics?: { googleAnalyticsId?: string }
  customScripts?: {
    headStart?: string
    headEnd?: string
    bodyStart?: string
    bodyEnd?: string
  }
  accessibility?: { addBypassLinks?: boolean }
}

function figmaSiteConfiguration(config: FigmaSiteConfiguration): Plugin {
  const clean = (value: string | undefined) => value?.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '') || ''
  const escape = (value: string) =>
    value.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  const title = config.title ?? 'Figma Make App'
  const description = config.description ?? ''
  const socialImage = config.openGraph?.image ?? ''
  const language = clean(config.language) || 'en'
  const googleAnalyticsId = clean(config.analytics?.googleAnalyticsId)
  const robotsTxt = config.robots?.index === false ? 'User-agent: *\nDisallow: /\n' : ''

  return {
    name: 'figma-site-configuration',
    configureServer(server) {
      server.middlewares.use((req, res, next) => {
        if (!robotsTxt || req.url?.split('?')[0] !== '/robots.txt') return next()
        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8')
        res.end(robotsTxt)
      })
    },
    generateBundle() {
      if (robotsTxt) this.emitFile({ type: 'asset', fileName: 'robots.txt', source: robotsTxt })
    },
    transformIndexHtml: {
      order: 'pre',
      handler(html) {
        let transformed = html
        transformed = transformed.replace('<!-- figma:lang -->', language)
        transformed = transformed.replace('<!-- figma:title -->', escape(title))
        transformed = transformed.replace(
          '<!-- figma:head-start -->',
          config.customScripts?.headStart ?? '',
        )
        transformed = transformed.replace(
          '<!-- figma:head-end -->',
          config.customScripts?.headEnd ?? '',
        )
        transformed = transformed.replace(
          '<!-- figma:body-start -->',
          config.customScripts?.bodyStart ?? '',
        )
        transformed = transformed.replace(
          '<!-- figma:body-end -->',
          config.customScripts?.bodyEnd ?? '',
        )

        const tags: HtmlTagDescriptor[] = []
        if (description)
          tags.push({
            tag: 'meta',
            attrs: { name: 'description', content: description },
            injectTo: 'head',
          })
        if (config.robots?.index === false)
          tags.push({
            tag: 'meta',
            attrs: { name: 'robots', content: 'noindex, nofollow' },
            injectTo: 'head',
          })
        if (config.icons?.icon)
          tags.push({
            tag: 'link',
            attrs: { rel: 'icon', href: config.icons.icon },
            injectTo: 'head',
          })
        if (title)
          tags.push({
            tag: 'meta',
            attrs: { property: 'og:title', content: title },
            injectTo: 'head',
          })
        if (description)
          tags.push({
            tag: 'meta',
            attrs: { property: 'og:description', content: description },
            injectTo: 'head',
          })
        if (socialImage)
          tags.push(
            {
              tag: 'meta',
              attrs: { property: 'og:image', content: socialImage },
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
            {
              tag: 'meta',
              attrs: { name: 'twitter:card', content: 'summary_large_image' },
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
            {
              tag: 'meta',
              attrs: { name: 'twitter:image', content: socialImage },
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
          )
        if (googleAnalyticsId) {
          tags.push(
            {
              tag: 'script',
              attrs: {
                async: true,
                src: `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${googleAnalyticsId}`,
              },
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
            {
              tag: 'script',
              children: `window.dataLayer=window.dataLayer||[];function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments)};gtag('js',new Date());gtag('config',${JSON.stringify(googleAnalyticsId)});`,
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
          )
        }

        if (config.accessibility?.addBypassLinks) {
          tags.push(
            {
              tag: 'style',
              children: `
  .figma-bypass-link {
    position: fixed;
    top: 8px;
    left: 8px;
    z-index: 2147483647;
    transform: translateY(-150%);
    border-radius: 6px;
    background: #111827;
    color: #fff;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    font: 600 14px/1.2 system-ui, sans-serif;
    text-decoration: none;
  }
  .figma-bypass-link:focus {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
`,
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
            {
              tag: 'a',
              attrs: { class: 'figma-bypass-link', href: '#root' },
              children: 'Skip to content',
              injectTo: 'body-prepend',
            },
          )
        }
        return { html: transformed, tags }
      },
    },
  }
}

function figmaErrorOverlayReplay(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'figma-error-overlay-replay',
    apply: 'serve',
    configureServer(server) {
      let lastError: object | null = null
      const originalSend = server.ws.send.bind(server.ws) as (...args: any[]) => void
      server.ws.send = ((...args: any[]) => {
        const payload = args[0]
        if (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload)) {
          const type = (payload as { type?: string }).type
          if (type === 'error') lastError = payload as object
          if (type === 'update' || type === 'full-reload') lastError = null
        }
        return originalSend(...args)
      }) as typeof server.ws.send
      server.ws.on('connection', (socket) => {
        if (lastError !== null) socket.send(JSON.stringify(lastError))
      })
    },
  }
}

function figmaReactRefreshBoundaryFallback(): Plugin {
  const hadRefreshBoundary = new Map<string, boolean>()
  let sendFullReload: (() => void) | null = null

  return {
    name: 'figma-react-refresh-boundary-fallback',
    apply: 'serve',
    enforce: 'post',
    configureServer(server) {
      sendFullReload = () => server.ws.send({ type: 'full-reload', path: '*' })
    },
    transform(code, id) {
      if (!/\.[jt]sx?(?:\?|$)/.test(id) || id.includes('/node_modules/')) return null

      const moduleId = id.split('?')[0] ?? id
      const hasRefreshBoundary = code.includes('registerExportsForReactRefresh')
      const previousHadRefreshBoundary = hadRefreshBoundary.get(moduleId)
      hadRefreshBoundary.set(moduleId, hasRefreshBoundary)
      if (previousHadRefreshBoundary && !hasRefreshBoundary) {
        queueMicrotask(() => sendFullReload?.())
      }
      return null
    },
  }
}

function figmaMakeKitPlugin(options: { storiesGlob: string | string[] }): Plugin {
  const storiesGlob = Array.isArray(options.storiesGlob)
    ? options.storiesGlob
    : [options.storiesGlob]
  const virtualId = 'virtual:figma-stories'
  const resolvedId = '\0' + virtualId
  const html = `<!doctype html><html><body><div id="figma-make-kit-root"></div><script type="module">import { stories } from 'virtual:figma-stories';window.__FIGMA__=Object.assign(window.__FIGMA__??{},{stories});window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('figma.ready'))</script></body></html>`

  return {
    name: 'figma-make-kit',
    apply: 'serve',
    resolveId(id) {
      return id === virtualId ? resolvedId : null
    },
    load(id) {
      return id === resolvedId
        ? `export const stories = import.meta.glob(${JSON.stringify(storiesGlob)})`
        : null
    },
    configureServer(server) {
      server.middlewares.use(async (req, res, next) => {
        const url = req.url || ''
        if (url.split('?')[0] !== '/.figma/make/kit.html') return next()
        try {
          res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/html')
          res.end(await server.transformIndexHtml(url, html))
        } catch (error) {
          next(error as Error)
        }
      })
    },
  }
}

```
